  44: /*
  45:  * =========================================
  46:  * Error Normalization
  47:  * =========================================
  48:  */
  49: 
  50: function normalizeTrainingContextError(error) {
  51:   return {
  52:     message:
  53:       error?.publicMessage ||
  54:       error?.message ||
  55:       "An unexpected Training Library error occurred.",
  56: 
  57:     code: error?.code || "TRAINING_OPERATION_FAILED",
  58: 
  59:     status: Number(error?.status) || 500,
  60: 
  61:     errors: Array.isArray(error?.errors) ? error.errors : [],
  62: 
  63:     warnings: Array.isArray(error?.warnings) ? error.warnings : [],
  64: 
  65:     fieldErrors:
  66:       error?.fieldErrors && typeof error.fieldErrors === "object"
  67:         ? error.fieldErrors
  68:         : {},
  69: 
  70:     validation:
  71:       error?.validation && typeof error.validation === "object"
  72:         ? error.validation
  73:         : null,
  74: 
  75:     details:
  76:       error?.details && typeof error.details === "object"
  77:         ? error.details
  78:         : null,
  79: 
  80:     originalError: error,
  81:   };
  82: }
  83: 
  84: /*
  85:  * =========================================
  86:  * Library Sorting
  87:  * =========================================
  88:  */
  89: 
  90: function getTrainingDateValue(training) {
  91:   const value =
  92:     training?.dates?.endDate ||
  93:     training?.dates?.startDate ||
  94:     training?.updatedAt ||
  95:     "";
  96: 
  97:   const timestamp = new Date(value).getTime();
  98: 
  99:   return Number.isFinite(timestamp) ? timestamp : 0;
 100: }
 101: 
 102: function sortTrainingLibrary(trainingRecords = []) {
 103:   return [...trainingRecords].sort((firstTraining, secondTraining) => {
 104:     const firstCurrent =
 105:       firstTraining?.dates?.isCurrent === true ||
 106:       firstTraining?.completion?.status === "in-progress";
 107: 
 108:     const secondCurrent =
 109:       secondTraining?.dates?.isCurrent === true ||
 110:       secondTraining?.completion?.status === "in-progress";
 111: 
 112:     if (firstCurrent !== secondCurrent) {
 113:       return firstCurrent ? -1 : 1;
 114:     }
 115: 
 116:     const dateDifference =
 117:       getTrainingDateValue(secondTraining) -
 118:       getTrainingDateValue(firstTraining);
 119: 
 120:     if (dateDifference !== 0) {
 121:       return dateDifference;
 122:     }
 123: 
 124:     return String(firstTraining?.title || "").localeCompare(
 125:       String(secondTraining?.title || ""),
 126:       undefined,
 127:       {
 128:         sensitivity: "base",
 129:       },
 130:     );
 131:   });
 132: }

 140: export function TrainingDataProvider({ children }) {
 141:   const [trainingRecords, setTrainingRecords] = useState([]);
 142: 
 143:   const [isLoading, setIsLoading] = useState(true);
 144: 
 145:   const [loadStatus, setLoadStatus] = useState("idle");
 146: 
 147:   const [saveStatus, setSaveStatus] = useState("idle");
 148: 
 149:   const [error, setError] = useState(null);
 150: 
 151:   const [operation, setOperation] = useState(INITIAL_OPERATION);
 152: 
 153:   /*
 154:    * =========================================
 155:    * Status Management
 156:    * =========================================
 157:    */
 158: 
 159:   const clearError = useCallback(() => {
 160:     setError(null);
 161:   }, []);
 162: 
 163:   const resetSaveStatus = useCallback(() => {
 164:     setSaveStatus("idle");
 165:   }, []);
 166: 
 167:   const resetOperation = useCallback(() => {
 168:     setOperation(INITIAL_OPERATION);
 169:   }, []);
 170: 
 171:   /*
 172:    * =========================================
 173:    * Load Training Records
 174:    * =========================================
 175:    */
 176: 
 177:   const refreshTraining = useCallback(
 178:     async ({ includeArchived = true, showLoading = true } = {}) => {
 179:       try {
 180:         if (showLoading) {
 181:           setIsLoading(true);
 182:           setLoadStatus("loading");
 183:         }
 184: 
 185:         setError(null);
 186: 
 187:         const loadedRecords = await getTrainingRecordsService({
 188:           includeArchived,
 189:         });
 190: 
 191:         const nextRecords = sortTrainingLibrary(
 192:           Array.isArray(loadedRecords) ? loadedRecords : [],
 193:         );
 194: 
 195:         setTrainingRecords(nextRecords);
 196:         setLoadStatus("success");
 197: 
 198:         return nextRecords;
 199:       } catch (loadError) {
 200:         console.error("Unable to load training records:", loadError);
 201: 
 202:         setLoadStatus("error");
 203:         setError(normalizeTrainingContextError(loadError));
 204: 
 205:         throw loadError;
 206:       } finally {
 207:         if (showLoading) {
 208:           setIsLoading(false);
 209:         }
 210:       }
 211:     },
 212:     [],
 213:   );
 214: 
 215:   /*
 216:    * =========================================
 217:    * Initial Load
 218:    * =========================================
 219:    */
 220: 
 221:   useEffect(() => {
 222:     let isActive = true;
 223: 
 224:     const loadInitialTraining = async () => {
 225:       try {
 226:         setIsLoading(true);
 227:         setLoadStatus("loading");
 228:         setError(null);
 229: 
 230:         const loadedRecords = await getTrainingRecordsService({
 231:           includeArchived: true,
 232:         });
 233: 
 234:         if (!isActive) {
 235:           return;
 236:         }
 237: 
 238:         setTrainingRecords(
 239:           sortTrainingLibrary(
 240:             Array.isArray(loadedRecords) ? loadedRecords : [],
 241:           ),
 242:         );
 243: 
 244:         setLoadStatus("success");
 245:       } catch (loadError) {
 246:         if (!isActive) {
 247:           return;
 248:         }
 249: 
 250:         console.error("Unable to load training records:", loadError);
 251: 
 252:         setTrainingRecords([]);
 253:         setLoadStatus("error");
 254:         setError(normalizeTrainingContextError(loadError));
 255:       } finally {
 256:         if (isActive) {
 257:           setIsLoading(false);
 258:         }
 259:       }
 260:     };
 261: 
 262:     loadInitialTraining();
 263: 
 264:     return () => {
 265:       isActive = false;
 266:     };
 267:   }, []);
 268: 
 269:   /*
 270:    * =========================================
 271:    * Cross-Tab Synchronization
 272:    * =========================================
 273:    */
 274: 
 275:   useEffect(() => {
 276:     const handleStorageChange = (event) => {
 277:       if (
 278:         event.storageArea !== window.localStorage ||
 279:         event.key !== TRAINING_STORAGE_KEY
 280:       ) {
 281:         return;
 282:       }
 283: 
 284:       refreshTraining({
 285:         includeArchived: true,
 286:         showLoading: false,
 287:       }).catch(() => {
 288:         /*
 289:          * The context already records the error.
 290:          */
 291:       });
 292:     };
 293: 
 294:     window.addEventListener("storage", handleStorageChange);
 295: 
 296:     return () => {
 297:       window.removeEventListener("storage", handleStorageChange);
 298:     };
 299:   }, [refreshTraining]);

 311:       );
 312: 
 313:       if (existingTraining) {
 314:         return existingTraining;

 709:   );
 710: 
 711:   const pausedTrainingRecords = useMemo(
 712:     () =>
 713:       activeTrainingRecords.filter(
 714:         (training) => training.completion?.status === "paused",
 715:       ),
 716:     [activeTrainingRecords],
 717:   );
 718: 
 719:   const withdrawnTrainingRecords = useMemo(
 720:     () =>
 721:       activeTrainingRecords.filter(
 722:         (training) => training.completion?.status === "withdrawn",
 723:       ),
 724:     [activeTrainingRecords],
 725:   );
 726: 
 727:   const trainingById = useMemo(
 728:     () => new Map(trainingRecords.map((training) => [training.id, training])),
 729:     [trainingRecords],
 730:   );
 731: 
 732:   const trainingByType = useMemo(
 733:     () =>
 734:       activeTrainingRecords.reduce((groups, training) => {
 735:         const trainingType = training.trainingType || "other";
 736: 
 737:         if (!groups[trainingType]) {
 738:           groups[trainingType] = [];
 739:         }
 740: 
 741:         groups[trainingType].push(training);
 742: 
 743:         return groups;
 744:       }, {}),
 745:     [activeTrainingRecords],
 746:   );
 747: 
 748:   const statistics = useMemo(
 749:     () => ({
 750:       total: trainingRecords.length,
 751: 
 752:       active: activeTrainingRecords.length,
 753: 
 754:       archived: archivedTrainingRecords.length,
 755: 
 756:       planned: plannedTrainingRecords.length,
 757: 
 758:       inProgress: inProgressTrainingRecords.length,
 759: 
 760:       completed: completedTrainingRecords.length,
 761: 
 762:       paused: pausedTrainingRecords.length,
 763: 
 764:       withdrawn: withdrawnTrainingRecords.length,
 765: 
 766:       withCredentials: activeTrainingRecords.filter(
 767:         (training) =>
 768:           training.completion?.certificateEarned === true ||
 769:           Boolean(training.completion?.credentialId) ||
 770:           Boolean(training.completion?.credentialUrl) ||
 771:           (Array.isArray(training.certificationRelationships) &&
 772:             training.certificationRelationships.length > 0),
 773:       ).length,
 774: 
 775:       withSkills: activeTrainingRecords.filter(
 776:         (training) =>
 777:           Array.isArray(training.skillRelationships) &&
 778:           training.skillRelationships.length > 0,
 779:       ).length,
 780: 
 781:       withDocuments: activeTrainingRecords.filter(
 782:         (training) =>
 783:           Array.isArray(training.supportingDocuments) &&
 784:           training.supportingDocuments.length > 0,
 785:       ).length,
 786:     }),
 787:     [
 788:       trainingRecords,
 789:       activeTrainingRecords,
 790:       archivedTrainingRecords,
 791:       plannedTrainingRecords,
 792:       inProgressTrainingRecords,
 793:       completedTrainingRecords,
 794:       pausedTrainingRecords,
 795:       withdrawnTrainingRecords,
 796:     ],
 797:   );
 798: 
 799:   /*
 800:    * =========================================
 801:    * Context Value
 802:    * =========================================
 803:    */
 804: 
 805:   const contextValue = useMemo(
 806:     () => ({
 807:       /*
 808:        * Collections
 809:        */
 810: 
 811:       trainingRecords,
 812: 
 813:       activeTrainingRecords,
 814:       archivedTrainingRecords,
 815: 
 816:       plannedTrainingRecords,
 817:       inProgressTrainingRecords,
 818:       completedTrainingRecords,
 819:       pausedTrainingRecords,
 820:       withdrawnTrainingRecords,
 821: 
 822:       trainingById,
 823:       trainingByType,
 824: 
 825:       statistics,
 826: 
 827:       /*
 828:        * Status
 829:        */
 830: 
 831:       isLoading,
 832:       loadStatus,
 833:       saveStatus,
 834:       operation,
 835:       error,
 836: 
 837:       /*
 838:        * Read Operations
 839:        */
 840: 
 841:       refreshTraining,
 842:       getTrainingById,
 843: 
 844:       /*
 845:        * Write Operations
 846:        */
 847: 
 848:       createTraining,
 849:       updateTraining,
 850:       archiveTraining,
 851:       restoreTraining,
 852:       deleteTraining,
 853:       replaceTrainingRecords,
 854: 
 855:       /*
 856:        * Validation
 857:        */
 858: 
 859:       validateTrainingDraft,
 860: 
 861:       /*
 862:        * Status Management
 863:        */
 864: 
 865:       clearError,
 866:       resetSaveStatus,
 867:       resetOperation,
 868:     }),
 869:     [
 870:       trainingRecords,
 871:       activeTrainingRecords,
 872:       archivedTrainingRecords,
 873:       plannedTrainingRecords,
 874:       inProgressTrainingRecords,
 875:       completedTrainingRecords,
 876:       pausedTrainingRecords,
 877:       withdrawnTrainingRecords,
 878:       trainingById,
 879:       trainingByType,
 880:       statistics,
 881:       isLoading,
 882:       loadStatus,
 883:       saveStatus,
 884:       operation,
 885:       error,
 886:       refreshTraining,
 887:       getTrainingById,
 888:       createTraining,
 889:       updateTraining,
 890:       archiveTraining,
 891:       restoreTraining,
 892:       deleteTraining,
 893:       replaceTrainingRecords,
 894:       validateTrainingDraft,
 895:       clearError,
 896:       resetSaveStatus,
 897:       resetOperation,
 898:     ],
 899:   );
 900: 
 901:   return (
 902:     <TrainingDataContext.Provider value={contextValue}>
 903:       {children}
 904:     </TrainingDataContext.Provider>
 905:   );
 906: }
 907: 
 908: /*
 909:  * =========================================
 910:  * Context Hook
 911:  * =========================================
 912:  */
 913: 
 914: export function useTrainingData() {
 915:   const context = useContext(TrainingDataContext);
 916: 
 917:   if (!context) {
 918:     throw new Error(
 919:       "useTrainingData must be used inside TrainingDataProvider.",
 920:     );
 921:   }
 922: 
 923:   return context;
 924: }

  22: /*
  23:  * =========================================
  24:  * ID Creation
  25:  * =========================================
  26:  */
  27: 
  28: export function createTrainingId(prefix = "training") {
  29:   if (
  30:     typeof crypto !== "undefined" &&
  31:     typeof crypto.randomUUID === "function"
  32:   ) {
  33:     return `${prefix}-${crypto.randomUUID()}`;
  34:   }
  35: 
  36:   return `${prefix}-${Date.now()}-${Math.random().toString(36).slice(2, 10)}`;
  37: }
  38: 
  39: /*
  40:  * =========================================
  41:  * Primitive Normalization
  42:  * =========================================
  43:  */
  44: 
  45: function normalizeText(value, maximumLength = Infinity) {
  46:   if (typeof value !== "string") {
  47:     return "";
  48:   }
  49: 
  50:   return value.trim().slice(0, maximumLength);
  51: }
  52: 
  53: function normalizeBoolean(value, defaultValue = false) {
  54:   return typeof value === "boolean" ? value : defaultValue;
  55: }
  56: 
  57: function normalizeNullableNumber(value, maximumValue = Infinity) {
  58:   if (value === "" || value === null || value === undefined) {
  59:     return null;
  60:   }
  61: 
  62:   const numberValue = Number(value);
  63: 
  64:   if (!Number.isFinite(numberValue)) {
  65:     return null;
  66:   }
  67: 
  68:   return Math.min(maximumValue, Math.max(0, numberValue));
  69: }
  70: 
  71: function normalizeDate(value) {
  72:   if (typeof value !== "string") {
  73:     return "";
  74:   }
  75: 
  76:   const text = value.trim();
  77: 
  78:   if (!/^\d{4}-(0[1-9]|1[0-2])(?:-(0[1-9]|[12]\d|3[01]))?$/.test(text)) {
  79:     return "";
  80:   }
  81: 
  82:   return text;
  83: }
  84: 
  85: function normalizeIdentifierList(values, maximumItems) {
  86:   if (!Array.isArray(values)) {
  87:     return [];
  88:   }
  89: 
  90:   return [
  91:     ...new Set(
  92:       values
  93:         .map((value) =>
  94:           typeof value === "string"
  95:             ? value.trim()
  96:             : String(value?.id || "").trim(),
  97:         )
  98:         .filter(Boolean),
  99:     ),
 100:   ].slice(0, maximumItems);
 101: }

 109: export function createTrainingInstructor(value = {}) {
 110:   const source =
 111:     typeof value === "string"
 112:       ? {
 113:           name: value,
 114:         }
 115:       : value && typeof value === "object"
 116:         ? value
 117:         : {};
 118: 
 119:   return {
 120:     id:
 121:       normalizeText(source.id, 200) || createTrainingId("training-instructor"),
 122: 
 123:     name: normalizeText(source.name, TRAINING_FIELD_LIMITS.instructorName),
 124: 
 125:     title: normalizeText(source.title, TRAINING_FIELD_LIMITS.instructorTitle),
 126: 
 127:     organization: normalizeText(
 128:       source.organization,
 129:       TRAINING_FIELD_LIMITS.instructorOrganization,
 130:     ),
 131: 
 132:     order:
 133:       Number.isInteger(source.order) && source.order >= 0 ? source.order : 0,
 134:   };
 135: }

 137: function normalizeTrainingInstructors(values) {
 138:   if (!Array.isArray(values)) {
 139:     return [];
 140:   }
 141: 
 142:   return values
 143:     .map(createTrainingInstructor)
 144:     .filter((instructor) => instructor.name)
 145:     .slice(0, TRAINING_FIELD_LIMITS.maximumInstructors)
 146:     .map((instructor, index) => ({
 147:       ...instructor,
 148:       order: index,
 149:     }));
 150: }

 251: export function createTrainingSkillRelationship(value = {}) {
 252:   const source =
 253:     typeof value === "string"
 254:       ? {
 255:           nameSnapshot: value,
 256:         }
 257:       : value && typeof value === "object"
 258:         ? value
 259:         : {};
 260: 
 261:   return {
 262:     id: normalizeText(source.id, 200) || createTrainingId("training-skill"),
 263: 
 264:     skillId: normalizeText(source.skillId || source.profileSkillId, 200),
 265: 
 266:     nameSnapshot: normalizeText(
 267:       source.nameSnapshot || source.name || source.label,
 268:       TRAINING_FIELD_LIMITS.skillName,
 269:     ),
 270: 
 271:     categorySnapshot: normalizeText(
 272:       source.categorySnapshot || source.category,
 273:       TRAINING_FIELD_LIMITS.skillCategory,
 274:     ),
 275: 
 276:     typeSnapshot: normalizeText(
 277:       source.typeSnapshot || source.type,
 278:       TRAINING_FIELD_LIMITS.skillType,
 279:     ),
 280: 
 281:     order:
 282:       Number.isInteger(source.order) && source.order >= 0 ? source.order : 0,
 283:   };

 286: function normalizeTrainingSkillRelationships(values) {
 287:   if (!Array.isArray(values)) {
 288:     return [];
 289:   }
 290: 
 291:   const normalizedRelationships = values
 292:     .map(createTrainingSkillRelationship)
 293:     .filter(
 294:       (relationship) => relationship.skillId || relationship.nameSnapshot,
 295:     );
 296: 
 297:   const uniqueRelationships = normalizedRelationships.filter(
 298:     (relationship, index, collection) => {
 299:       const identity = (
 300:         relationship.skillId || relationship.nameSnapshot
 301:       ).toLocaleLowerCase();
 302: 
 303:       return (
 304:         collection.findIndex((candidate) => {
 305:           const candidateIdentity = (
 306:             candidate.skillId || candidate.nameSnapshot
 307:           ).toLocaleLowerCase();
 308: 
 309:           return candidateIdentity === identity;
 310:         }) === index
 311:       );
 312:     },
 313:   );
 314: 
 315:   return uniqueRelationships
 316:     .slice(0, TRAINING_FIELD_LIMITS.maximumSkills)
 317:     .map((relationship, index) => ({
 318:       ...relationship,
 319:       order: index,
 320:     }));
 321: }

 323: /*
 324:  * =========================================
 325:  * Certification Relationship
 326:  * =========================================
 327:  */
 328: 
 329: export function createTrainingCertificationRelationship(value = {}, order = 0) {
 330:   const source =
 331:     typeof value === "string"
 332:       ? {
 333:           certificationId: value,
 334:         }
 335:       : value && typeof value === "object" && !Array.isArray(value)
 336:         ? value
 337:         : {};
 338: 
 339:   const snapshot =
 340:     source.snapshot &&
 341:     typeof source.snapshot === "object" &&
 342:     !Array.isArray(source.snapshot)
 343:       ? source.snapshot
 344:       : {};
 345: 
 346:   /*
 347:    * When a complete Certification record is supplied,
 348:    * its id is the Certification id.
 349:    *
 350:    * When an existing relationship is supplied,
 351:    * source.id is the relationship id and
 352:    * source.certificationId identifies the Certification.
 353:    */
 354: 
 355:   const certificationId = normalizeText(
 356:     source.certificationId ||
 357:       source.credentialId ||
 358:       source.certificationRecordId ||
 359:       source.id,
 360:     200,
 361:   );
 362: 
 363:   const relationshipId =
 364:     normalizeText(
 365:       source.relationshipId || (source.certificationId ? source.id : ""),
 366:       200,
 367:     ) || createTrainingId("training-certification");
 368: 
 369:   return {
 370:     id: relationshipId,
 371: 
 372:     certificationId,
 373: 
 374:     order:
 375:       Number.isInteger(source.order) && source.order >= 0
 376:         ? source.order
 377:         : order,
 378: 
 379:     snapshot: {
 380:       name: normalizeText(
 381:         snapshot.name ||
 382:           snapshot.title ||
 383:           source.certificationName ||
 384:           source.name ||
 385:           source.title,
 386:         160,
 387:       ),
 388: 
 389:       issuingOrganizationName: normalizeText(
 390:         snapshot.issuingOrganizationName ||
 391:           snapshot.issuerName ||
 392:           source.issuingOrganizationName ||
 393:           source.issuerName ||
 394:           source.issuingOrganization?.name,
 395:         160,
 396:       ),
 397: 
 398:       certificationType: normalizeText(
 399:         snapshot.certificationType || snapshot.type || source.certificationType,
 400:         100,
 401:       ),
 402: 
 403:       credentialState: normalizeText(
 404:         snapshot.credentialState ||
 405:           snapshot.state ||
 406:           source.credentialState ||
 407:           source.credential?.state,
 408:         100,
 409:       ),
 410: 
 411:       issueDate: normalizeDate(
 412:         snapshot.issueDate || source.issueDate || source.dates?.issueDate,
 413:       ),
 414: 
 415:       expirationDate: normalizeDate(
 416:         snapshot.expirationDate ||
 417:           source.expirationDate ||
 418:           source.dates?.expirationDate,
 419:       ),
 420: 
 421:       doesNotExpire: normalizeBoolean(
 422:         snapshot.doesNotExpire ??
 423:           source.doesNotExpire ??
 424:           source.dates?.doesNotExpire,
 425:         false,
 426:       ),
 427:     },
 428:   };
 429: }
 430: 
 431: function normalizeTrainingCertificationRelationships(values) {
 432:   if (!Array.isArray(values)) {
 433:     return [];
 434:   }
 435: 
 436:   const usedCertificationIds = new Set();
 437: 
 438:   return values.reduce((relationships, value) => {
 439:     const relationship = createTrainingCertificationRelationship(
 440:       value,
 441:       relationships.length,
 442:     );
 443: 
 444:     if (!relationship.certificationId) {
 445:       return relationships;
 446:     }
 447: 
 448:     const comparisonId = relationship.certificationId.toLocaleLowerCase();
 449: 
 450:     if (usedCertificationIds.has(comparisonId)) {
 451:       return relationships;
 452:     }
 453: 
 454:     if (
 455:       relationships.length >= TRAINING_FIELD_LIMITS.maximumRelatedCertifications
 456:     ) {
 457:       return relationships;
 458:     }
 459: 
 460:     usedCertificationIds.add(comparisonId);
 461: 
 462:     relationships.push({
 463:       ...relationship,
 464:       order: relationships.length,
 465:     });
 466: 
 467:     return relationships;
 468:   }, []);
 469: }
 470: 
 471: export function getTrainingCertificationRelationships(training) {
 472:   return normalizeTrainingCertificationRelationships(
 473:     training?.certificationRelationships || training?.relatedCertificationIds,
 474:   );
 475: }
 476: 
 477: export function getTrainingCertificationIds(training) {
 478:   return getTrainingCertificationRelationships(training).map(
 479:     (relationship) => relationship.certificationId,
 480:   );
 481: }
 482: 
 483: export function isCertificationLinkedToTraining(training, certificationId) {
 484:   const normalizedCertificationId = normalizeText(certificationId, 200);
 485: 
 486:   if (!normalizedCertificationId) {
 487:     return false;
 488:   }
 489: 
 490:   return getTrainingCertificationRelationships(training).some(
 491:     (relationship) =>
 492:       relationship.certificationId === normalizedCertificationId,
 493:   );
 494: }

 496: /*
 497:  * =========================================
 498:  * Supporting Document
 499:  * =========================================
 500:  */
 501: 
 502: export function createTrainingDocument(value = {}) {
 503:   const source =
 504:     value && typeof value === "object" && !Array.isArray(value) ? value : {};
 505: 
 506:   return {
 507:     id: normalizeText(source.id, 200) || createTrainingId("training-document"),
 508: 
 509:     documentType: isValidTrainingDocumentType(source.documentType)
 510:       ? source.documentType
 511:       : "supporting-evidence",
 512: 
 513:     name: normalizeText(source.name || source.title, 250),
 514: 
 515:     description: normalizeText(source.description, 1000),
 516: 
 517:     fileName: normalizeText(source.fileName, 500),
 518: 
 519:     mimeType: normalizeText(source.mimeType || source.fileType, 200),
 520: 
 521:     fileSize: normalizeNullableNumber(source.fileSize),
 522: 
 523:     storageProvider:
 524:       normalizeText(source.storageProvider, 100) ||
 525:       (source.storageKey ? "indexed-db" : ""),
 526: 
 527:     storageKey: normalizeText(source.storageKey, 500),
 528: 
 529:     fileUrl: normalizeText(source.fileUrl || source.url, 2000),
 530: 
 531:     uploadedAt: normalizeText(source.uploadedAt, 100),
 532: 
 533:     visibility: isValidDocumentVisibility(source.visibility)
 534:       ? source.visibility
 535:       : "private",
 536: 
 537:     order:
 538:       Number.isInteger(source.order) && source.order >= 0 ? source.order : 0,
 539:   };
 540: }
 541: 
 542: function normalizeTrainingDocuments(values) {
 543:   if (!Array.isArray(values)) {
 544:     return [];
 545:   }
 546: 
 547:   const normalizedDocuments = values
 548:     .map(createTrainingDocument)
 549:     .filter(
 550:       (document) =>
 551:         document.name ||
 552:         document.fileName ||
 553:         document.storageKey ||
 554:         document.fileUrl,
 555:     );
 556: 
 557:   const uniqueDocuments = normalizedDocuments.filter(
 558:     (document, index, collection) => {
 559:       const identity =
 560:         document.storageKey ||
 561:         document.fileUrl ||
 562:         document.id ||
 563:         document.fileName;
 564: 
 565:       return (
 566:         collection.findIndex((candidate) => {
 567:           const candidateIdentity =
 568:             candidate.storageKey ||
 569:             candidate.fileUrl ||
 570:             candidate.id ||
 571:             candidate.fileName;
 572: 
 573:           return candidateIdentity === identity;
 574:         }) === index
 575:       );
 576:     },
 577:   );
 578: 
 579:   return uniqueDocuments
 580:     .slice(0, TRAINING_FIELD_LIMITS.maximumDocuments)
 581:     .map((document, index) => ({
 582:       ...document,
 583:       order: index,
 584:     }));
 585: }

 587: /*
 588:  * =========================================
 589:  * Empty Training
 590:  * =========================================
 591:  */
 592: 
 593: export function createEmptyTraining() {
 594:   const timestamp = new Date().toISOString();
 595: 
 596:   return {
 597:     modelVersion: TRAINING_MODEL_VERSION,
 598: 
 599:     id: createTrainingId(),
 600: 
 601:     title: "",
 602: 
 603:     trainingType: "professional-course",
 604: 
 605:     provider: {
 606:       name: "",
 607:       type: "",
 608:       website: "",
 609:     },
 610: 
 611:     delivery: {
 612:       format: "in-person",
 613: 
 614:       location: {
 615:         city: "",
 616:         stateRegion: "",
 617:         country: "",
 618:         displayValue: "",
 619:       },
 620:     },
 621: 
 622:     dates: {
 623:       startDate: "",
 624:       endDate: "",
 625:       isCurrent: false,
 626:     },
 627: 
 628:     completion: {
 629:       status: "completed",
 630:       durationHours: null,
 631: 
 632:       certificateEarned: false,
 633: 
 634:       credentialId: "",
 635:       credentialUrl: "",
 636:     },
 637: 
 638:     description: "",
 639: 
 640:     instructors: [],
 641: 
 642:     topics: [],
 643: 
 644:     learningOutcomes: [],
 645: 
 646:     skillRelationships: [],
 647: 
 648:     supportingDocuments: [],
 649: 
 650:     relatedEducationIds: [],
 651: 
 652:     /*
 653:      * Canonical Certification relationships.
 654:      *
 655:      * Each relationship includes the Certification ID
 656:      * and a readable snapshot so Training can still show
 657:      * useful information if the Certification record is
 658:      * temporarily unavailable.
 659:      */
 660: 
 661:     certificationRelationships: [],
 662: 
 663:     /*
 664:      * Legacy compatibility field.
 665:      *
 666:      * This is derived from certificationRelationships
 667:      * during normalization and should not be edited
 668:      * independently by new components.
 669:      */
 670: 
 671:     relatedCertificationIds: [],
 672: 
 673:     relatedExperienceIds: [],
 674: 
 675:     relatedProjectIds: [],
 676: 
 677:     visibility: {
 678:       showProvider: true,
 679:       showProviderWebsite: true,
 680:       showDeliveryFormat: true,
 681:       showLocation: true,
 682:       showDates: true,
 683:       showDuration: true,
 684:       showDescription: true,
 685:       showInstructors: true,
 686:       showTopics: true,
 687:       showLearningOutcomes: true,
 688:       showSkills: true,
 689:       showCredential: true,
 690:       showCertifications: true,
 691:       showSupportingDocuments: false,
 692:     },
 693: 
 694:     privateInformation: {
 695:       notes: "",
 696:     },
 697: 
 698:     source: "manual",
 699: 
 700:     sourceContext: "",
 701: 
 702:     status: "active",
 703: 
 704:     createdAt: timestamp,
 705:     updatedAt: timestamp,
 706:   };
 707: }
 708: 
 709: /*
 710:  * =========================================
 711:  * Normalize Training
 712:  * =========================================
 713:  */
 714: 
 715: export function normalizeTraining(value = {}) {
 716:   const defaults = createEmptyTraining();
 717: 
 718:   const source =
 719:     value && typeof value === "object" && !Array.isArray(value) ? value : {};
 720: 
 721:   const provider =
 722:     source.provider &&
 723:     typeof source.provider === "object" &&
 724:     !Array.isArray(source.provider)
 725:       ? source.provider
 726:       : {};
 727: 
 728:   const delivery =
 729:     source.delivery &&
 730:     typeof source.delivery === "object" &&
 731:     !Array.isArray(source.delivery)
 732:       ? source.delivery
 733:       : {};
 734: 
 735:   const location =
 736:     delivery.location &&
 737:     typeof delivery.location === "object" &&
 738:     !Array.isArray(delivery.location)
 739:       ? delivery.location
 740:       : source.location &&
 741:           typeof source.location === "object" &&
 742:           !Array.isArray(source.location)
 743:         ? source.location
 744:         : {};
 745: 
 746:   const dates =
 747:     source.dates &&
 748:     typeof source.dates === "object" &&
 749:     !Array.isArray(source.dates)
 750:       ? source.dates
 751:       : {};
 752: 
 753:   const completion =
 754:     source.completion &&
 755:     typeof source.completion === "object" &&
 756:     !Array.isArray(source.completion)
 757:       ? source.completion
 758:       : {};
 759: 
 760:   const visibility =
 761:     source.visibility &&
 762:     typeof source.visibility === "object" &&
 763:     !Array.isArray(source.visibility)
 764:       ? source.visibility
 765:       : {};
 766: 
 767:   const privateInformation =
 768:     source.privateInformation &&
 769:     typeof source.privateInformation === "object" &&
 770:     !Array.isArray(source.privateInformation)
 771:       ? source.privateInformation
 772:       : {};
 773: 
 774:   const trainingType = isValidTrainingType(source.trainingType)
 775:     ? source.trainingType
 776:     : defaults.trainingType;
 777: 
 778:   const providerType = isValidTrainingProviderType(provider.type ?? "")
 779:     ? (provider.type ?? "")
 780:     : defaults.provider.type;
 781: 
 782:   const deliveryFormat = isValidTrainingDeliveryFormat(delivery.format)
 783:     ? delivery.format
 784:     : defaults.delivery.format;
 785: 
 786:   const completionStatus = isValidTrainingCompletionStatus(completion.status)
 787:     ? completion.status
 788:     : defaults.completion.status;
 789: 
 790:   const trainingStatus = isValidTrainingStatus(source.status)
 791:     ? source.status
 792:     : defaults.status;
 793: 
 794:   const trainingSource = isValidTrainingSource(source.source)
 795:     ? source.source
 796:     : defaults.source;
 797: 
 798:   const isCurrent =
 799:     completionStatus === "in-progress" ||
 800:     normalizeBoolean(dates.isCurrent, false);
 801: 
 802:   /*
 803:    * Version 1 records stored only Certification IDs.
 804:    * Version 2 stores complete relationship objects.
 805:    */
 806: 
 807:   const certificationRelationshipSource = Array.isArray(
 808:     source.certificationRelationships,
 809:   )
 810:     ? source.certificationRelationships
 811:     : source.relatedCertificationIds;
 812: 
 813:   const certificationRelationships =
 814:     normalizeTrainingCertificationRelationships(
 815:       certificationRelationshipSource,
 816:     );
 817: 
 818:   return {
 819:     modelVersion: TRAINING_MODEL_VERSION,
 820: 
 821:     id: normalizeText(source.id, 200) || defaults.id,
 822: 
 823:     title: normalizeText(
 824:       source.title || source.name,
 825:       TRAINING_FIELD_LIMITS.title,
 826:     ),
 827: 
 828:     trainingType,
 829: 
 830:     provider: {
 831:       name: normalizeText(
 832:         provider.name || source.providerName || source.organizationName,
 833:         TRAINING_FIELD_LIMITS.providerName,
 834:       ),
 835: 
 836:       type: providerType,
 837: 
 838:       website: normalizeText(
 839:         provider.website || source.providerWebsite,
 840:         TRAINING_FIELD_LIMITS.providerWebsite,
 841:       ),
 842:     },
 843: 
 844:     delivery: {
 845:       format: deliveryFormat,
 846: 
 847:       location: {
 848:         city: normalizeText(location.city, TRAINING_FIELD_LIMITS.city),
 849: 
 850:         stateRegion: normalizeText(
 851:           location.stateRegion || location.state,
 852:           TRAINING_FIELD_LIMITS.stateRegion,
 853:         ),
 854: 
 855:         country: normalizeText(location.country, TRAINING_FIELD_LIMITS.country),
 856: 
 857:         displayValue: normalizeText(
 858:           location.displayValue ||
 859:             source.locationValue ||
 860:             (typeof source.location === "string" ? source.location : ""),
 861:           TRAINING_FIELD_LIMITS.locationDisplayValue,
 862:         ),
 863:       },
 864:     },
 865: 
 866:     dates: {
 867:       startDate: normalizeDate(dates.startDate || source.startDate),
 868: 
 869:       endDate: isCurrent ? "" : normalizeDate(dates.endDate || source.endDate),
 870: 
 871:       isCurrent,
 872:     },
 873: 
 874:     completion: {
 875:       status: completionStatus,
 876: 
 877:       durationHours: normalizeNullableNumber(
 878:         completion.durationHours ?? source.durationHours,
 879:         TRAINING_FIELD_LIMITS.maximumDurationHours,
 880:       ),
 881: 
 882:       certificateEarned: normalizeBoolean(completion.certificateEarned, false),
 883: 
 884:       credentialId: normalizeText(
 885:         completion.credentialId || source.credentialId,
 886:         TRAINING_FIELD_LIMITS.credentialId,
 887:       ),
 888: 
 889:       credentialUrl: normalizeText(
 890:         completion.credentialUrl || source.credentialUrl,
 891:         TRAINING_FIELD_LIMITS.credentialUrl,
 892:       ),
 893:     },
 894: 
 895:     description: normalizeText(
 896:       source.description || source.overview,
 897:       TRAINING_FIELD_LIMITS.description,
 898:     ),
 899: 
 900:     instructors: normalizeTrainingInstructors(source.instructors),
 901: 
 902:     topics: normalizeTrainingTopics(source.topics || source.curriculum),
 903: 
 904:     learningOutcomes: normalizeTrainingOutcomes(
 905:       source.learningOutcomes || source.outcomes,
 906:     ),
 907: 
 908:     skillRelationships: normalizeTrainingSkillRelationships(
 909:       source.skillRelationships || source.skills,
 910:     ),
 911: 
 912:     supportingDocuments: normalizeTrainingDocuments(
 913:       source.supportingDocuments || source.documents || source.attachments,
 914:     ),
 915: 
 916:     relatedEducationIds: normalizeIdentifierList(
 917:       source.relatedEducationIds,
 918:       TRAINING_FIELD_LIMITS.maximumRelatedEducation,
 919:     ),
 920: 
 921:     certificationRelationships,
 922: 
 923:     /*
 924:      * Keep the previous ID list synchronized for
 925:      * services or saved records still using model v1.
 926:      */
 927: 
 928:     relatedCertificationIds: certificationRelationships.map(
 929:       (relationship) => relationship.certificationId,
 930:     ),
 931: 
 932:     relatedExperienceIds: normalizeIdentifierList(
 933:       source.relatedExperienceIds,
 934:       TRAINING_FIELD_LIMITS.maximumRelatedExperiences,
 935:     ),
 936: 
 937:     relatedProjectIds: normalizeIdentifierList(
 938:       source.relatedProjectIds,
 939:       TRAINING_FIELD_LIMITS.maximumRelatedProjects,
 940:     ),
 941: 
 942:     visibility: {
 943:       showProvider: normalizeBoolean(
 944:         visibility.showProvider,
 945:         defaults.visibility.showProvider,
 946:       ),
 947: 
 948:       showProviderWebsite: normalizeBoolean(
 949:         visibility.showProviderWebsite,
 950:         defaults.visibility.showProviderWebsite,
 951:       ),
 952: 
 953:       showDeliveryFormat: normalizeBoolean(
 954:         visibility.showDeliveryFormat,
 955:         defaults.visibility.showDeliveryFormat,
 956:       ),
 957: 
 958:       showLocation: normalizeBoolean(
 959:         visibility.showLocation,
 960:         defaults.visibility.showLocation,
 961:       ),
 962: 
 963:       showDates: normalizeBoolean(
 964:         visibility.showDates,
 965:         defaults.visibility.showDates,
 966:       ),
 967: 
 968:       showDuration: normalizeBoolean(
 969:         visibility.showDuration,
 970:         defaults.visibility.showDuration,
 971:       ),
 972: 
 973:       showDescription: normalizeBoolean(
 974:         visibility.showDescription,
 975:         defaults.visibility.showDescription,
 976:       ),
 977: 
 978:       showInstructors: normalizeBoolean(
 979:         visibility.showInstructors,
 980:         defaults.visibility.showInstructors,
 981:       ),
 982: 
 983:       showTopics: normalizeBoolean(
 984:         visibility.showTopics,
 985:         defaults.visibility.showTopics,
 986:       ),
 987: 
 988:       showLearningOutcomes: normalizeBoolean(
 989:         visibility.showLearningOutcomes,
 990:         defaults.visibility.showLearningOutcomes,
 991:       ),
 992: 
 993:       showSkills: normalizeBoolean(
 994:         visibility.showSkills,
 995:         defaults.visibility.showSkills,
 996:       ),
 997: 
 998:       showCredential: normalizeBoolean(
 999:         visibility.showCredential,
1000:         defaults.visibility.showCredential,
1001:       ),
1002: 
1003:       showCertifications: normalizeBoolean(
1004:         visibility.showCertifications,
1005:         defaults.visibility.showCertifications,
1006:       ),
1007: 
1008:       showSupportingDocuments: normalizeBoolean(
1009:         visibility.showSupportingDocuments,
1010:         defaults.visibility.showSupportingDocuments,
1011:       ),
1012:     },
1013: 
1014:     privateInformation: {
1015:       notes: normalizeText(
1016:         privateInformation.notes || source.privateNotes,
1017:         TRAINING_FIELD_LIMITS.privateNotes,
1018:       ),
1019:     },
1020: 
1021:     source: trainingSource,
1022: 
1023:     sourceContext: normalizeText(
1024:       source.sourceContext,
1025:       TRAINING_FIELD_LIMITS.sourceContext,
1026:     ),
1027: 
1028:     status: trainingStatus,
1029: 
1030:     createdAt: normalizeText(source.createdAt, 100) || defaults.createdAt,
1031: 
1032:     updatedAt: normalizeText(source.updatedAt, 100) || defaults.updatedAt,
1033:   };
1034: }
1035: 
1036: /*
1037:  * =========================================
1038:  * Create Training
1039:  * =========================================
1040:  */
1041: 
1042: export function createTraining(values = {}) {
1043:   const timestamp = new Date().toISOString();
1044: 
1045:   return normalizeTraining({
1046:     ...values,
1047: 
1048:     id: normalizeText(values?.id, 200) || createTrainingId(),
1049: 
1050:     createdAt: normalizeText(values?.createdAt, 100) || timestamp,
1051: 
1052:     updatedAt: timestamp,
1053:   });
1054: }
1055: 
1056: /*
1057:  * =========================================
1058:  * Update Training
1059:  * =========================================
1060:  */
1061: 
1062: export function updateTrainingModel(currentTraining, updates = {}) {
1063:   const safeUpdates = updates && typeof updates === "object" ? updates : {};
1064: 
1065:   return normalizeTraining({
1066:     ...currentTraining,
1067:     ...safeUpdates,
1068: 
1069:     provider: {
1070:       ...currentTraining?.provider,
1071:       ...safeUpdates.provider,
1072:     },
1073: 
1074:     delivery: {
1075:       ...currentTraining?.delivery,
1076:       ...safeUpdates.delivery,
1077: 
1078:       location: {
1079:         ...currentTraining?.delivery?.location,
1080:         ...safeUpdates.delivery?.location,
1081:       },
1082:     },
1083: 
1084:     dates: {
1085:       ...currentTraining?.dates,
1086:       ...safeUpdates.dates,
1087:     },
1088: 
1089:     completion: {
1090:       ...currentTraining?.completion,
1091:       ...safeUpdates.completion,
1092:     },
1093: 
1094:     certificationRelationships:
1095:       safeUpdates.certificationRelationships ??
1096:       currentTraining?.certificationRelationships ??
1097:       currentTraining?.relatedCertificationIds ??
1098:       [],
1099: 
1100:     visibility: {
1101:       ...currentTraining?.visibility,
1102:       ...safeUpdates.visibility,
1103:     },
1104: 
1105:     privateInformation: {
1106:       ...currentTraining?.privateInformation,
1107:       ...safeUpdates.privateInformation,
1108:     },
1109: 
1110:     id: currentTraining?.id,
1111: 
1112:     createdAt: currentTraining?.createdAt,
1113: 
1114:     updatedAt: new Date().toISOString(),
1115:   });
1116: }
1117: 
1118: /*
1119:  * =========================================
1120:  * Normalize Collection
1121:  * =========================================
1122:  */
1123: 
1124: export function normalizeTrainingCollection(values) {
1125:   if (!Array.isArray(values)) {
1126:     return [];
1127:   }
1128: 
1129:   const normalizedTraining = values.map(normalizeTraining);
1130: 
1131:   return normalizedTraining.filter(
1132:     (training, index, collection) =>
1133:       collection.findIndex((candidate) => candidate.id === training.id) ===
1134:       index,
1135:   );
1136: }
1137: 
1138: /*
1139:  * =========================================
1140:  * Public Training
1141:  * =========================================
1142:  */
1143: 
1144: export function createPublicTraining(value) {
1145:   const training = normalizeTraining(value);
1146: 
1147:   const {
1148:     privateInformation,
1149:     sourceContext,
1150:     relatedCertificationIds,
1151:     ...publicTraining
1152:   } = training;
1153: 
1154:   return {
1155:     ...publicTraining,
1156: 
1157:     certificationRelationships: publicTraining.visibility.showCertifications
1158:       ? publicTraining.certificationRelationships.map((relationship) => ({
1159:           id: relationship.id,
1160:           certificationId: relationship.certificationId,
1161:           order: relationship.order,
1162: 
1163:           snapshot: {
1164:             name: relationship.snapshot.name,
1165: 
1166:             issuingOrganizationName:
1167:               relationship.snapshot.issuingOrganizationName,
1168: 
1169:             certificationType: relationship.snapshot.certificationType,
1170: 
1171:             credentialState: relationship.snapshot.credentialState,
1172: 
1173:             issueDate: relationship.snapshot.issueDate,
1174: 
1175:             expirationDate: relationship.snapshot.expirationDate,
1176: 
1177:             doesNotExpire: relationship.snapshot.doesNotExpire,
1178:           },
1179:         }))
1180:       : [],
1181: 
1182:     supportingDocuments: publicTraining.visibility.showSupportingDocuments
1183:       ? publicTraining.supportingDocuments.filter(
1184:           (document) => document.visibility === "public",
1185:         )
1186:       : [],
1187:   };
1188: }

 246:       await Promise.resolve(synchronizeCertifications());

 248:       /*
 249:        * TrainingDataContext displays the error.

 251:     }

 263:     const providerName = getTrainingProviderName(training);
 264:

 266:       `Permanently delete "${trainingTitle}" from "${providerName}"?\n\n` +
 267:         "Its supporting document files will also be removed. This action cannot be undone.",
 268:     );
 269:

 277:       await Promise.resolve(synchronizeCertifications());

 282:     } catch {
 283:       /*
 284:        * TrainingDataContext displays the error.
 285:        */
 286:     }

 316:        * TrainingDataContext displays the error.

 318:     });

 510:         storageKey: storedFile.storageKey,
 511:         fileUrl: "",
 512:         uploadedAt: storedFile.uploadedAt,
 513:         visibility,
 514:         order: safeDocuments.length,
 515:       });
 516: 
 517:       await onChange?.(
 518:         normalizeDocumentOrder([...safeDocuments, trainingDocument]),
 519:       );
 520: 
 521:       resetEditor();
 522:     } catch (error) {
 523:       if (storedFile?.storageKey) {
 524:         try {
 525:           await deleteStoredDocumentFile(storedFile.storageKey);
 526:         } catch {
 527:           /*
 528:            * Preserve the original upload error.
 529:            */
 530:         }
 531:       }
 532: 
 533:       setEditorError(
 534:         error?.publicMessage ||
 535:           error?.message ||
 536:           "The document could not be uploaded.",
 537:       );
 538:     } finally {
 539:       setIsUploading(false);
 540:     }
 541:   };
 542: 
 543:   /*
 544:    * =========================================
 545:    * Link Certification
 546:    * =========================================
 547:    */
 548: 
 549:   const handleLinkCertification = async () => {
 550:     setEditorError("");
 551: 
 552:     if (typeof onCertificationChange !== "function") {
 553:       setEditorError(
 554:         "Certification linking is not available in this Training form.",
 555:       );
 556:       return;
 557:     }
 558: 
 559:     if (!selectedCertificationId) {
 560:       setEditorError("Select a Certification from the library.");
 561:       return;
 562:     }
 563: 
 564:     if (hasReachedCertificationMaximum) {
 565:       setEditorError(
 566:         `Link no more than ${maximumCertifications} Certifications.`,
 567:       );
 568:       return;
 569:     }
 570: 
 571:     const certification = certificationById.get(selectedCertificationId);
 572: 
 573:     if (!certification) {
 574:       setEditorError("The selected Certification could not be found.");
 575:       return;
 576:     }
 577: 
 578:     if (linkedCertificationIds.has(certification.id)) {
 579:       setEditorError("This Certification is already linked.");
 580:       return;
 581:     }
 582: 
 583:     try {
 584:       setWorkingCertificationId(certification.id);
 585: 
 586:       await onCertificationChange(certification, true);
 587: 
 588:       setSelectedCertificationId("");
 589:     } catch (error) {
 590:       setEditorError(
 591:         error?.publicMessage ||
 592:           error?.message ||
 593:           "The Certification could not be linked.",
 594:       );
 595:     } finally {
 596:       setWorkingCertificationId("");
 597:     }
 598:   };
 599: 
 600:   /*
 601:    * =========================================
 602:    * Create Certification
 603:    * =========================================
 604:    */
 605: 
 606:   const handleCreateCertification = async () => {
 607:     setEditorError("");
 608: 
 609:     if (typeof onCreateCertification !== "function") {
 610:       setEditorError(
 611:         "Creating a Certification is not available in this Training form.",
 612:       );
 613:       return;
 614:     }
 615: 
 616:     if (typeof onCertificationChange !== "function") {
 617:       setEditorError(
 618:         "The new Certification cannot be linked to this Training.",
 619:       );
 620:       return;
 621:     }
 622: 
 623:     const normalizedName = certificationName.trim();
 624:     const normalizedIssuerName = certificationIssuerName.trim();
 625:     const normalizedCredentialId = certificationCredentialId.trim();
 626:     const normalizedVerificationUrl = certificationVerificationUrl.trim();
 627: 
 628:     if (!normalizedName) {
 629:       setEditorError("Enter the Certification name.");
 630:       return;
 631:     }
 632: 
 633:     if (!normalizedIssuerName) {
 634:       setEditorError("Enter the issuing organization.");
 635:       return;
 636:     }
 637: 
 638:     if (!certificationIssueDate) {
 639:       setEditorError("Enter the Certification issue date.");
 640:       return;
 641:     }
 642: 
 643:     if (
 644:       !certificationDoesNotExpire &&
 645:       certificationExpirationDate &&
 646:       certificationExpirationDate < certificationIssueDate
 647:     ) {
 648:       setEditorError(
 649:         "The expiration date cannot be earlier than the issue date.",
 650:       );
 651:       return;
 652:     }
 653: 
 654:     if (normalizedVerificationUrl) {
 655:       try {
 656:         const url = new URL(normalizedVerificationUrl);
 657: 
 658:         if (!["http:", "https:"].includes(url.protocol)) {
 659:           throw new Error("Unsupported URL protocol.");
 660:         }
 661:       } catch {
 662:         setEditorError(
 663:           "Enter a valid verification URL beginning with http:// or https://.",
 664:         );
 665:         return;
 666:       }
 667:     }
 668: 
 669:     if (hasReachedCertificationMaximum) {
 670:       setEditorError(
 671:         `Link no more than ${maximumCertifications} Certifications.`,
 672:       );
 673:       return;
 674:     }
 675: 
 676:     try {
 677:       setIsCreatingCertification(true);
 678: 
 679:       const createdCertification = await onCreateCertification({
 680:         certificationData: {
 681:           name: normalizedName,
 682: 
 683:           certificationType: "professional",
 684: 
 685:           issuingOrganization: {
 686:             name: normalizedIssuerName,
 687:             type: "other",
 688:             website: "",
 689:           },
 690: 
 691:           dates: {
 692:             issueDate: certificationIssueDate,
 693: 
 694:             expirationDate: certificationDoesNotExpire
 695:               ? ""
 696:               : certificationExpirationDate,
 697: 
 698:             lastRenewedDate: "",
 699:             nextRenewalDate: "",
 700: 
 701:             doesNotExpire: certificationDoesNotExpire,
 702:           },
 703: 
 704:           credential: {
 705:             state: "active",
 706:             credentialId: normalizedCredentialId,
 707:             verificationUrl: normalizedVerificationUrl,
 708:           },
 709: 
 710:           description: "Certification created from the Training editor.",
 711: 
 712:           relatedRecords: {
 713:             trainingRelationships: [],
 714:             educationIds: [],
 715:           },
 716: 
 717:           visibility: {
 718:             showIssuingOrganization: true,
 719:             showIssuerWebsite: false,
 720:             showDates: true,
 721:             showExpirationDate: true,
 722:             showCredentialId: Boolean(normalizedCredentialId),
 723:             showVerificationUrl: Boolean(normalizedVerificationUrl),
 724:             showDescription: true,
 725:             showSkills: true,
 726:             showSupportingDocuments: Boolean(certificationFile),
 727:           },
 728: 
 729:           source: "training",
 730: 
 731:           sourceContext: trainingId
 732:             ? `Created from Training draft ${trainingId}.`
 733:             : "Created from the Training editor.",
 734: 
 735:           status: "active",
 736:         },
 737: 
 738:         file: certificationFile,
 739:       });
 740: 
 741:       await onCertificationChange(createdCertification, true);
 742: 
 743:       resetCertificationCreator();
 744: 
 745:       /*
 746:        * Return to the existing-Certification selector.
 747:        * The newly created Certification is already
 748:        * linked and will appear in the linked list.
 749:        */
 750: 
 751:       setDocumentSource("certification");
 752:     } catch (error) {
 753:       setEditorError(
 754:         error?.publicMessage ||
 755:           error?.message ||
 756:           "The Certification could not be created.",
 757:       );
 758:     } finally {
 759:       setIsCreatingCertification(false);
 760:     }
 761:   };
 762: 
 763:   /*
 764:    * =========================================
 765:    * Unlink Certification
 766:    * =========================================
 767:    */
 768: 
 769:   const handleUnlinkCertification = async (linkedCertification) => {
 770:     if (typeof onCertificationChange !== "function") {
 771:       setEditorError(
 772:         "Certification unlinking is not available in this Training form.",
 773:       );
 774:       return;
 775:     }
 776: 
 777:     const shouldUnlink = window.confirm(
 778:       `Remove the link to "${linkedCertification.name}"?\n\n` +
 779:         "The Certification record and its files will remain in the Certification Library.",
 780:     );
 781: 
 782:     if (!shouldUnlink) {
 783:       return;
 784:     }
 785: 
 786:     try {
 787:       setEditorError("");
 788:       setWorkingCertificationId(linkedCertification.id);
 789: 
 790:       await onCertificationChange(
 791:         linkedCertification.certification || {
 792:           id: linkedCertification.id,
 793:         },
 794:         false,
 795:       );
 796:     } catch (error) {
 797:       setEditorError(
 798:         error?.publicMessage ||
 799:           error?.message ||
 800:           "The Certification could not be unlinked.",
 801:       );
 802:     } finally {
 803:       setWorkingCertificationId("");
 804:     }
 805:   };
 806: 
 807:   /*
 808:    * =========================================
 809:    * Resolve Manual Document URL
 810:    * =========================================
 811:    */
 812: 
 813:   const getDocumentUrl = async (trainingDocument) => {
 814:     if (trainingDocument.fileUrl) {
 815:       return {
 816:         url: trainingDocument.fileUrl,
 817:         temporary: false,
 818:       };
 819:     }
 820: 
 821:     if (!trainingDocument.storageKey) {
 822:       const error = new Error(
 823:         "The document does not have a valid storage location.",
 824:       );
 825: 
 826:       error.publicMessage = "The document file is unavailable.";
 827: 
 828:       throw error;
 829:     }
 830: 
 831:     const url = await createStoredDocumentUrl(trainingDocument.storageKey);
 832: 
 833:     return {
 834:       url,
 835:       temporary: true,
 836:     };
 837:   };
 838: 
 839:   /*
 840:    * =========================================
 841:    * Preview Manual Document
 842:    * =========================================
 843:    */
 844: 
 845:   const handlePreview = async (trainingDocument) => {
 846:     try {
 847:       setEditorError("");
 848:       setWorkingDocumentId(trainingDocument.id);
 849: 
 850:       const { url, temporary } = await getDocumentUrl(trainingDocument);
 851: 
 852:       const previewWindow = window.open(url, "_blank", "noopener,noreferrer");
 853: 
 854:       if (!previewWindow) {
 855:         if (temporary) {
 856:           revokeDocumentUrl(url);
 857:         }
 858: 
 859:         throw new Error("The browser blocked the document preview window.");
 860:       }
 861: 
 862:       if (temporary) {
 863:         window.setTimeout(() => {
 864:           revokeDocumentUrl(url);
 865:         }, 60000);
 866:       }
 867:     } catch (error) {
 868:       setEditorError(
 869:         error?.publicMessage ||
 870:           error?.message ||
 871:           "The document could not be opened.",
 872:       );
 873:     } finally {
 874:       setWorkingDocumentId("");
 875:     }
 876:   };
 877: 
 878:   /*
 879:    * =========================================
 880:    * Download Manual Document
 881:    * =========================================
 882:    */
 883: 
 884:   const handleDownload = async (trainingDocument) => {
 885:     try {
 886:       setEditorError("");
 887:       setWorkingDocumentId(trainingDocument.id);
 888: 
 889:       const { url, temporary } = await getDocumentUrl(trainingDocument);
 890: 
 891:       const link = window.document.createElement("a");
 892: 
 893:       link.href = url;
 894:       link.download =
 895:         trainingDocument.fileName || trainingDocument.name || "document";
 896:       link.rel = "noopener";
 897: 
 898:       window.document.body.appendChild(link);
 899:       link.click();
 900:       link.remove();
 901: 
 902:       if (temporary) {
 903:         window.setTimeout(() => {
 904:           revokeDocumentUrl(url);
 905:         }, 1000);
 906:       }
 907:     } catch (error) {
 908:       setEditorError(
 909:         error?.publicMessage ||
 910:           error?.message ||
 911:           "The document could not be downloaded.",
 912:       );
 913:     } finally {
 914:       setWorkingDocumentId("");
 915:     }
 916:   };
 917: 
 918:   /*
 919:    * =========================================
 920:    * Remove Manual Document
 921:    * =========================================
 922:    */
 923: 
 924:   const handleRemove = async (trainingDocument) => {
 925:     const shouldRemove = window.confirm(
 926:       `Remove "${trainingDocument.name}"?\n\n` +
 927:         "The uploaded file will also be removed from browser storage.",
 928:     );
 929: 
 930:     if (!shouldRemove) {
 931:       return;
 932:     }
 933: 
 934:     try {
 935:       setEditorError("");
 936:       setWorkingDocumentId(trainingDocument.id);
 937: 
 938:       if (
 939:         trainingDocument.storageProvider === "indexed-db" &&
 940:         trainingDocument.storageKey
 941:       ) {
 942:         await deleteStoredDocumentFile(trainingDocument.storageKey);
 943:       }
 944: 
 945:       const remainingDocuments = safeDocuments.filter(
 946:         (candidate) => candidate.id !== trainingDocument.id,
 947:       );
 948: 
 949:       await onChange?.(normalizeDocumentOrder(remainingDocuments));
 950:     } catch (error) {
 951:       setEditorError(
 952:         error?.publicMessage ||
 953:           error?.message ||
 954:           "The document could not be removed.",
 955:       );
 956:     } finally {
 957:       setWorkingDocumentId("");
 958:     }
 959:   };
 960: 
 961:   /*
 962:    * =========================================
 963:    * Reorder Manual Documents
 964:    * =========================================
 965:    */
 966: 
 967:   const handleMove = (documentId, direction) => {
 968:     const currentIndex = orderedDocuments.findIndex(
 969:       (document) => document.id === documentId,
 970:     );
 971: 
 972:     if (currentIndex === -1) {
 973:       return;
 974:     }
 975: 
 976:     const targetIndex =
 977:       direction === "up" ? currentIndex - 1 : currentIndex + 1;
 978: 
 979:     if (targetIndex < 0 || targetIndex >= orderedDocuments.length) {
 980:       return;
 981:     }
 982: 
 983:     const reorderedDocuments = [...orderedDocuments];
 984: 
 985:     const [movedDocument] = reorderedDocuments.splice(currentIndex, 1);
 986: 
 987:     reorderedDocuments.splice(targetIndex, 0, movedDocument);
 988: 
 989:     onChange?.(normalizeDocumentOrder(reorderedDocuments));
 990:   };
 991: 
 992:   return (
 993:     <section
 994:       className="training-form-section training-document-editor"
 995:       aria-labelledby={sectionTitleId}
 996:     >
 997:       <header className="training-form-section-header">
 998:         <span aria-hidden="true" />
 999: 
1000:         <div>
1001:           <small>Supporting Evidence</small>
1002: 
1003:           <h3 id={sectionTitleId}>Documents and Certifications</h3>
1004: 
1005:           <p>
1006:             Upload a Training document manually or link a Certification that
1007:             already exists in your Certification Library.
1008:           </p>
1009:         </div>
1010:       </header>
1011: 
1012:       <div className="training-document-editor-notice">
1013:         <span aria-hidden="true">â“˜</span>
1014: 
1015:         <p>
1016:           Linked Certification files remain owned by the Certification Library.
1017:           Linking a Certification does not create a duplicate file.
1018:         </p>
1019:       </div>
1020: 
1021:       <div className="training-document-editor-source">
1022:         <label htmlFor={sourceSelectId}>Add Supporting Evidence</label>
1023: 
1024:         <select
1025:           id={sourceSelectId}
1026:           value={documentSource}
1027:           onChange={(event) => {
1028:             setDocumentSource(event.target.value);
1029:             setEditorError("");
1030:           }}
1031:           disabled={isDisabled}
1032:         >
1033:           <option value="manual">Upload a document manually</option>
1034:           <option value="certification">
1035:             Choose from Certification Library
1036:           </option>
1037:           <option value="create-certification">
1038:             Create a new Certification
1039:           </option>
1040:         </select>
1041:       </div>
1042: 
1043:       {documentSource === "manual" && (
1044:         <div className="training-document-editor-form">
1045:           <div className="training-document-editor-field training-document-editor-wide">
1046:             <label htmlFor={fileInputId}>
1047:               Select Document
1048:               <small> Required</small>
1049:             </label>
1050: 
1051:             <input
1052:               ref={fileInputRef}
1053:               id={fileInputId}
1054:               type="file"
1055:               accept={DOCUMENT_ACCEPT_VALUE}
1056:               onChange={handleFileChange}
1057:               disabled={isDisabled || hasReachedDocumentMaximum}
1058:             />
1059: 
1060:             <small>
1061:               PDF, Word, OpenDocument, text, JPG, PNG, WebP, GIF, BMP, or HEIC.
1062:               Maximum{" "}
1063:               {formatFileSize(DOCUMENT_FILE_LIMITS.maximumFileSizeBytes)}.
1064:             </small>
1065:           </div>
1066: 
1067:           {selectedFile && (
1068:             <div className="training-document-editor-selected training-document-editor-wide">
1069:               <span aria-hidden="true">â—‡</span>
1070: 
1071:               <div>
1072:                 <strong>{selectedFile.name}</strong>
1073: 
1074:                 <small>
1075:                   {getDocumentFormatLabel(selectedFile)} Â·{" "}
1076:                   {formatFileSize(selectedFile.size)}
1077:                 </small>
1078:               </div>
1079:             </div>
1080:           )}
1081: 
1082:           <div className="training-document-editor-field">
1083:             <label htmlFor={nameInputId}>
1084:               Document Name
1085:               <small> Required</small>
1086:             </label>
1087: 
1088:             <input
1089:               id={nameInputId}
1090:               type="text"
1091:               value={documentName}
1092:               onChange={(event) => {
1093:                 setDocumentName(event.target.value);
1094:                 setEditorError("");
1095:               }}
1096:               maxLength={DOCUMENT_FILE_LIMITS.documentName}
1097:               placeholder="Training Completion Certificate"
1098:               disabled={isDisabled}
1099:             />
1100:           </div>
1101: 
1102:           <div className="training-document-editor-field">
1103:             <label htmlFor={typeSelectId}>Document Type</label>
1104: 
1105:             <select
1106:               id={typeSelectId}
1107:               value={documentType}
1108:               onChange={(event) => {
1109:                 setDocumentType(event.target.value);
1110:                 setEditorError("");
1111:               }}
1112:               disabled={isDisabled}
1113:             >
1114:               {TRAINING_DOCUMENT_TYPE_OPTIONS.map((option) => (
1115:                 <option key={option.value} value={option.value}>
1116:                   {option.label}
1117:                 </option>
1118:               ))}
1119:             </select>
1120:           </div>
1121: 
1122:           <div className="training-document-editor-field">
1123:             <label htmlFor={visibilitySelectId}>Visibility</label>
1124: 
1125:             <select
1126:               id={visibilitySelectId}
1127:               value={visibility}
1128:               onChange={(event) => {
1129:                 setVisibility(event.target.value);
1130:                 setEditorError("");
1131:               }}
1132:               disabled={isDisabled}
1133:             >
1134:               {DOCUMENT_VISIBILITY_OPTIONS.map((option) => (
1135:                 <option key={option.value} value={option.value}>
1136:                   {option.label}
1137:                 </option>
1138:               ))}
1139:             </select>
1140:           </div>
1141: 
1142:           <div className="training-document-editor-field training-document-editor-wide">
1143:             <label htmlFor={descriptionInputId}>
1144:               Description
1145:               <small> Optional</small>
1146:             </label>
1147: 
1148:             <textarea
1149:               id={descriptionInputId}
1150:               rows={3}
1151:               value={description}
1152:               onChange={(event) => {
1153:                 setDescription(event.target.value);
1154:                 setEditorError("");
1155:               }}
1156:               maxLength={DOCUMENT_FILE_LIMITS.description}
1157:               placeholder="Describe what this document verifies."
1158:               disabled={isDisabled}
1159:             />
1160: 
1161:             <small className="training-document-editor-character-count">
1162:               {description.length}/{DOCUMENT_FILE_LIMITS.description}
1163:             </small>
1164:           </div>
1165: 
1166:           <footer className="training-document-editor-form-footer">
1167:             <small>
1168:               {safeDocuments.length}/{maximumDocuments} documents
1169:             </small>
1170: 
1171:             <div>
1172:               {selectedFile && (
1173:                 <button
1174:                   type="button"
1175:                   onClick={resetEditor}
1176:                   disabled={isDisabled}
1177:                 >
1178:                   Clear
1179:                 </button>
1180:               )}
1181: 
1182:               <button
1183:                 type="button"
1184:                 className="training-document-editor-add"
1185:                 onClick={handleAddDocument}
1186:                 disabled={
1187:                   isDisabled || hasReachedDocumentMaximum || !selectedFile
1188:                 }
1189:               >
1190:                 {isUploading ? "Uploading..." : "+ Add Document"}
1191:               </button>
1192:             </div>
1193:           </footer>
1194:         </div>
1195:       )}
1196: 
1197:       {documentSource === "certification" && (
1198:         <div className="training-document-editor-certification-picker">
1199:           <div className="training-document-editor-field">
1200:             <label htmlFor={certificationSelectId}>
1201:               Saved Certification
1202:               <small> Optional</small>
1203:             </label>
1204: 
1205:             <select
1206:               id={certificationSelectId}
1207:               value={selectedCertificationId}
1208:               onChange={(event) => {
1209:                 setSelectedCertificationId(event.target.value);
1210:                 setEditorError("");
1211:               }}
1212:               disabled={
1213:                 isDisabled ||
1214:                 isCertificationDataLoading ||
1215:                 availableCertifications.length === 0 ||
1216:                 hasReachedCertificationMaximum
1217:               }
1218:             >
1219:               <option value="">
1220:                 {isCertificationDataLoading
1221:                   ? "Loading Certifications..."
1222:                   : hasReachedCertificationMaximum
1223:                     ? "Maximum linked Certifications reached"
1224:                     : availableCertifications.length === 0
1225:                       ? "No available Certifications"
1226:                       : "Select a saved Certification"}
1227:               </option>
1228: 
1229:               {availableCertifications.map((certification) => (
1230:                 <option key={certification.id} value={certification.id}>
1231:                   {certification.name || "Unnamed Certification"}
1232:                   {" â€” "}
1233:                   {certification.issuingOrganization?.name || "Unknown issuer"}
1234:                 </option>
1235:               ))}
1236:             </select>
1237: 
1238:             <small>
1239:               Only active Certifications that are not already linked are
1240:               available.
1241:             </small>
1242:           </div>
1243: 
1244:           <button
1245:             type="button"
1246:             className="training-document-editor-link-certification"
1247:             onClick={handleLinkCertification}
1248:             disabled={
1249:               isDisabled ||
1250:               isCertificationDataLoading ||
1251:               hasReachedCertificationMaximum ||
1252:               !selectedCertificationId ||
1253:               Boolean(workingCertificationId)
1254:             }
1255:           >
1256:             {workingCertificationId ? "Linking..." : "+ Link Certification"}
1257:           </button>
1258:         </div>
1259:       )}
1260: 
1261:       {documentSource === "create-certification" && (
1262:         <div className="training-document-editor-create-certification">
1263:           <div className="training-document-editor-create-heading">
1264:             <div>
1265:               <small>Certification Library</small>
1266: 
1267:               <h4>Create a New Certification</h4>
1268: 
1269:               <p>
1270:                 This creates a separate Certification record and links it to
1271:                 this Training draft.
1272:               </p>
1273:             </div>
1274:           </div>
1275: 
1276:           <div className="training-document-editor-create-grid">
1277:             <div className="training-document-editor-field training-document-editor-wide">
1278:               <label htmlFor={certificationNameInputId}>

 155:       if (!current[field]) {
 156:         return current;
 157:       }

 160:         ...current,
 161:       };

 178:   };

 182:       ...current,
