 289:       ? formData.certificationRelationships
 290:       : [];
 291: 
 292:     const relationshipAlreadyExists = currentRelationships.some(
 293:       (relationship) =>
 294:         relationship?.certificationId === certificationId ||
 295:         relationship?.certificationRecordId === certificationId,
 296:     );
 297: 
 298:     if (shouldBeLinked && relationshipAlreadyExists) {
 299:       const error = new Error(
 300:         `Certification "${certificationId}" is already linked.`,
 301:       );
 302: 
 303:       error.publicMessage =
 304:         "This Certification is already linked to the Training.";
 305: 
 306:       throw error;
 307:     }
 308: 
 309:     const maximumCertifications =
 310:       TRAINING_FIELD_LIMITS.maximumRelatedCertifications ?? Infinity;
 311: 
 312:     if (
 313:       shouldBeLinked &&
 314:       currentRelationships.length >= maximumCertifications
 315:     ) {
 316:       const error = new Error(
 317:         `The Training cannot have more than ${maximumCertifications} linked Certifications.`,
 318:       );
 319: 
 320:       error.publicMessage = `Link no more than ${maximumCertifications} Certifications.`;
 321: 
 322:       throw error;
 323:     }
 324: 
 325:     setFormData((currentData) => {
 326:       const relationships = Array.isArray(
 327:         currentData.certificationRelationships,
 328:       )
 329:         ? currentData.certificationRelationships
 330:         : [];
 331: 
 332:       const remainingRelationships = relationships.filter(
 333:         (relationship) =>
 334:           relationship?.certificationId !== certificationId &&
 335:           relationship?.certificationRecordId !== certificationId,
 336:       );
 337: 
 338:       const nextRelationships = shouldBeLinked
 339:         ? [
 340:             ...remainingRelationships,
 341: 
 342:             createTrainingCertificationRelationship(
 343:               certification,
 344:               remainingRelationships.length,
 345:             ),
 346:           ]
 347:         : remainingRelationships;
 348: 
 349:       const orderedRelationships = nextRelationships.map(
 350:         (relationship, index) => ({
 351:           ...relationship,
 352:           order: index,
 353:         }),
 354:       );
 355: 
 356:       const credentialId =
 357:         typeof certification?.credential?.credentialId === "string"
 358:           ? certification.credential.credentialId.trim()
 359:           : typeof certification?.credential?.id === "string"
 360:             ? certification.credential.id.trim()
 361:             : "";
 362: 
 363:       const credentialUrl =
 364:         typeof certification?.credential?.verificationUrl === "string"
 365:           ? certification.credential.verificationUrl.trim()
 366:           : typeof certification?.credential?.url === "string"
 367:             ? certification.credential.url.trim()
 368:             : "";
 369: 
 370:       return {
 371:         ...currentData,
 372: 
 373:         certificationRelationships: orderedRelationships,
 374: 
 375:         /*
 376:          * Preserve this legacy collection until every
 377:          * consumer uses certificationRelationships.
 378:          */
 379: 
 380:         relatedCertificationIds: orderedRelationships
 381:           .map(
 382:             (relationship) =>
 383:               relationship?.certificationId ||
 384:               relationship?.certificationRecordId,
 385:           )
 386:           .filter(Boolean),
 387: 
 388:         completion: {
 389:           ...currentData.completion,
 390: 
 391:           /*
 392:            * Any linked Certification is evidence that
 393:            * this Training has a Certification relationship.
 394:            */
 395: 
 396:           certificateEarned: shouldBeLinked
 397:             ? true
 398:             : currentData.completion?.certificateEarned === true,
 399: 
 400:           /*
 401:            * Fill empty Training credential fields from
 402:            * the linked Certification without overwriting
 403:            * manually entered information.
 404:            */
 405: 
 406:           credentialId:
 407:             shouldBeLinked && !currentData.completion?.credentialId
 408:               ? credentialId
 409:               : currentData.completion?.credentialId || "",
 410: 
 411:           credentialUrl:
 412:             shouldBeLinked && !currentData.completion?.credentialUrl
 413:               ? credentialUrl
 414:               : currentData.completion?.credentialUrl || "",
 415:         },
 416:       };
 417:     });
 418: 
 419:     setFieldErrors((currentErrors) => {
 420:       const nextErrors = {
 421:         ...currentErrors,
 422:       };
 423: 
 424:       Object.keys(nextErrors).forEach((field) => {
 425:         if (
 426:           field === "certificationRelationships" ||
 427:           field === "relatedCertificationIds" ||
 428:           field === "completion.certificateEarned" ||
 429:           field === "completion.credentialId" ||
 430:           field === "completion.credentialUrl" ||
 431:           field.startsWith("certificationRelationships.")
 432:         ) {
 433:           delete nextErrors[field];
 434:         }
 435:       });
 436: 
 437:       return nextErrors;
 438:     });
 439: 
 440:     setSubmitError("");
 441:   };
 442: 
 443:   /*
 444:    * =========================================
 445:    * Submission
 446:    * =========================================
 447:    */
 448: 
 449:   const handleSubmit = async (event) => {
 450:     event.preventDefault();
 451: 
 452:     const validation = validateTraining(formData, {
 453:       certifications,
 454:     });
 455: 
 456:     setFieldErrors(validation.fieldErrors || {});
 457:     setWarnings(validation.warnings || []);
 458: 
 459:     if (!validation.isValid) {
 460:       const errorCount = validation.errors?.length || 0;
 461: 
 462:       setSubmitError(
 463:         `${errorCount} ${
 464:           errorCount === 1 ? "problem must" : "problems must"
 465:         } be corrected before saving.`,
 466:       );
 467: 
 468:       const firstError = validation.errors?.[0];
 469: 
 470:       if (firstError?.field) {
 471:         document
 472:           .querySelector(`[data-field="${firstError.field}"]`)
 473:           ?.scrollIntoView({
 474:             behavior: "smooth",
 475:             block: "center",
 476:           });
 477:       }
 478: 
 479:       return;
 480:     }
 481: 
 482:     try {
 483:       setSubmitError("");
 484: 
 485:       await onSubmit?.(formData);
 486:     } catch (error) {
 487:       setFieldErrors(error?.fieldErrors || {});
 488: 
 489:       setSubmitError(
 490:         error?.publicMessage ||
 491:           error?.message ||
 492:           "The training record could not be saved.",
 493:       );
 494:     }
 495:   };
 496: 
 497:   return (
 498:     <form className="training-form" onSubmit={handleSubmit} noValidate>
 499:       {/* Training Program */}
 500: 
 501:       <section className="training-form-section">
 502:         <SectionHeader
 503:           label="Program"
 504:           title="Training Program"
 505:           description="Enter the training title, type, and provider."
 506:         />
 507: 
 508:         <div className="training-form-grid">
 509:           <FormField
 510:             label="Training Title"
 511:             field="title"
 512:             error={fieldErrors.title}
 513:             required
 514:             wide
 515:           >
 516:             <input
 517:               type="text"
 518:               value={formData.title}
 519:               onChange={(event) => updateTopLevel("title", event.target.value)}
 520:               maxLength={TRAINING_FIELD_LIMITS.title}
 521:               placeholder="CompTIA A+ Technical Training"
 522:               disabled={isSaving}
 523:             />
 524:           </FormField>
 525: 
 526:           <FormField
 527:             label="Training Type"
 528:             field="trainingType"
 529:             error={fieldErrors.trainingType}
 530:             required
 531:           >
 532:             <select
 533:               value={formData.trainingType}
 534:               onChange={(event) =>
 535:                 updateTopLevel("trainingType", event.target.value)
 536:               }
 537:               disabled={isSaving}
 538:             >
 539:               {TRAINING_TYPE_OPTIONS.map((option) => (
 540:                 <option key={option.value} value={option.value}>
 541:                   {option.label}
 542:                 </option>
 543:               ))}
 544:             </select>
 545:           </FormField>
 546: 
 547:           <FormField
 548:             label="Provider Type"
 549:             field="provider.type"
 550:             error={fieldErrors["provider.type"]}
 551:           >
 552:             <select
 553:               value={formData.provider.type}
 554:               onChange={(event) =>
 555:                 updateNested("provider", "type", event.target.value)
 556:               }
 557:               disabled={isSaving}
 558:             >
 559:               {TRAINING_PROVIDER_TYPE_OPTIONS.map((option) => (
 560:                 <option
 561:                   key={option.value || "unspecified"}
 562:                   value={option.value}
 563:                 >
 564:                   {option.label}
 565:                 </option>
 566:               ))}
 567:             </select>
 568:           </FormField>
 569: 
 570:           <FormField
 571:             label="Provider Name"
 572:             field="provider.name"
 573:             error={fieldErrors["provider.name"]}
 574:             required
 575:           >
 576:             <input
 577:               type="text"
 578:               value={formData.provider.name}
 579:               onChange={(event) =>
 580:                 updateNested("provider", "name", event.target.value)
 581:               }
 582:               maxLength={TRAINING_FIELD_LIMITS.providerName}
 583:               placeholder="CompTIA"
 584:               disabled={isSaving}
 585:             />
 586:           </FormField>
 587: 
 588:           <FormField
 589:             label="Provider Website"
 590:             field="provider.website"
 591:             error={fieldErrors["provider.website"]}
 592:           >
 593:             <input
 594:               type="url"
 595:               value={formData.provider.website}
 596:               onChange={(event) =>
 597:                 updateNested("provider", "website", event.target.value)
 598:               }
 599:               maxLength={TRAINING_FIELD_LIMITS.providerWebsite}
 600:               placeholder="https://www.example.com"
 601:               disabled={isSaving}
 602:             />
 603:           </FormField>
 604:         </div>
 605:       </section>
 606: 
 607:       {/* Dates and Delivery */}
 608: 
 609:       <section className="training-form-section">
 610:         <SectionHeader
 611:           label="Timeline"
 612:           title="Dates and Delivery"
 613:           description="Describe when, where, and how the training was delivered."
 614:         />
 615: 
 616:         <div className="training-form-grid">
 617:           <FormField
 618:             label="Start Date"
 619:             field="dates.startDate"
 620:             error={fieldErrors["dates.startDate"]}
 621:             required
 622:           >
 623:             <input
 624:               type="month"
 625:               value={formData.dates.startDate}
 626:               onChange={(event) =>
 627:                 updateNested("dates", "startDate", event.target.value)

 630:             />
 631:           </FormField>

 688:                 updateDeepNested(

 698:             />
 699:           </FormField>
 700:

 705:           >
 706:             <input
 707:               type="text"
 708:               value={formData.delivery.location.city}
 709:               onChange={(event) =>
 710:                 updateDeepNested(
 711:                   "delivery",
 712:                   "location",
 713:                   "city",
 714:                   event.target.value,
 715:                 )
 716:               }
 717:               maxLength={TRAINING_FIELD_LIMITS.city}
 718:               disabled={isSaving}
 719:             />
 720:           </FormField>
 721: 
 722:           <FormField
 723:             label="State or Region"

 725:             error={fieldErrors["delivery.location.stateRegion"]}
 726:           >
 727:             <input
 728:               type="text"
 729:               value={formData.delivery.location.stateRegion}
 730:               onChange={(event) =>
 731:                 updateDeepNested(
 732:                   "delivery",

 736:                 )

 752:                 updateDeepNested(

 789:               {TRAINING_COMPLETION_STATUS_OPTIONS.map((option) => (

 791:                   {option.label}
 792:                 </option>
 793:               ))}
 794:             </select>
 795:           </FormField>
 796: 
 797:           <FormField
 798:             label="Duration in Hours"
 799:             field="completion.durationHours"
 800:             error={fieldErrors["completion.durationHours"]}
 801:           >
 802:             <input
 803:               type="number"
 804:               min="0"
 805:               max={TRAINING_FIELD_LIMITS.maximumDurationHours}
 806:               step="0.25"
 807:               value={formData.completion.durationHours ?? ""}
 808:               onChange={(event) =>
 809:                 updateNested("completion", "durationHours", event.target.value)
 810:               }
 811:               placeholder="40"
 812:               disabled={isSaving}
 813:             />
 814:           </FormField>
 815: 
 816:           <label className="training-form-checkbox training-form-wide">

 821:                 updateNested(
 822:                   "completion",
 823:                   "certificateEarned",
 824:                   event.target.checked,
 825:                 )
 826:               }
 827:               disabled={isSaving}
 828:             />
 829: 
 830:             <span>A certificate or credential was earned</span>
 831:           </label>
 832: 
 833:           <FormField
 834:             label="Credential ID"
 835:             field="completion.credentialId"
 836:             error={fieldErrors["completion.credentialId"]}
 837:           >
 838:             <input
 839:               type="text"
 840:               value={formData.completion.credentialId}
 841:               onChange={(event) =>
 842:                 updateNested("completion", "credentialId", event.target.value)
 843:               }
 844:               maxLength={TRAINING_FIELD_LIMITS.credentialId}
 845:               disabled={isSaving}
 846:             />
 847:           </FormField>
 848: 
 849:           <FormField
 850:             label="Credential URL"

 853:           >

 855:               type="url"
 856:               value={formData.completion.credentialUrl}
 857:               onChange={(event) =>
 858:                 updateNested("completion", "credentialUrl", event.target.value)

 861:               placeholder="https://www.example.com/verify"
 862:               disabled={isSaving}
 863:             />
 864:           </FormField>

 895:       {/* Instructors */}

 986: 
 987:       <section className="training-form-section">

 989:           label="Management"

 991:           description="Control visibility, source information, private notes, and archive status."
 992:         />
 993: 
 994:         <div className="training-form-visibility">
 995:           {[
 996:             ["showProvider", "Show training provider"],
 997:             ["showProviderWebsite", "Show provider website"],
 998:             ["showDeliveryFormat", "Show delivery format"],
 999:             ["showLocation", "Show training location"],
1000:             ["showDates", "Show training dates"],
1001:             ["showDuration", "Show training duration"],
1002:             ["showDescription", "Show training description"],
1003:             ["showInstructors", "Show instructors"],
1004:             ["showTopics", "Show topics and curriculum"],
1005:             ["showLearningOutcomes", "Show learning outcomes"],
1006:             ["showSkills", "Show related skills"],
1007:             ["showCredential", "Show credential information"],
1008:             ["showCertifications", "Show linked Certifications"],
1009:             ["showSupportingDocuments", "Show supporting documents"],
1010:           ].map(([field, label]) => (
1011:             <label key={field} className="training-form-checkbox">

1017:                 }

1019:               />
1020: 
1021:               <span>{label}</span>
1022:             </label>

1024:         </div>
1025: 
1026:         <div className="training-form-grid">
1027:           <FormField label="Source" field="source" error={fieldErrors.source}>
1028:             <select
1029:               value={formData.source}
1030:               onChange={(event) => updateTopLevel("source", event.target.value)}
1031:               disabled={isSaving}
1032:             >
1033:               {TRAINING_SOURCE_OPTIONS.map((option) => (

  32: function getText(value) {
  33:   return typeof value === "string" ? value.trim() : "";
  34: }
  35: 
  36: function getOrderedItems(values) {
  37:   return Array.isArray(values)
  38:     ? [...values].sort(
  39:         (first, second) => (first.order ?? 0) - (second.order ?? 0),
  40:       )
  41:     : [];

  47:   if (!website) {
  48:     return "";
  49:   }
  50: 
  51:   return /^https?:\/\//i.test(website) ? website : `https://${website}`;

  54: function formatTrainingLocation(training) {
  55:   const location = training?.delivery?.location || {};
  56: 
  57:   return (
  58:     getText(location.displayValue) ||
  59:     [location.city, location.stateRegion, location.country]

  61:       .filter(Boolean)
  62:       .join(", ")

  66: function getCredentialStateLabel(state) {
  67:   const normalizedState = getText(state);
  68: 
  69:   if (!normalizedState) {
  70:     return "Unspecified";
  71:   }
  72: 
  73:   return normalizedState
  74:     .split("-")
  75:     .filter(Boolean)
  76:     .map(
  77:       (word) =>
  78:         word.charAt(0).toLocaleUpperCase() +
  79:         word.slice(1).toLocaleLowerCase(),
  80:     )
  81:     .join(" ");
  82: }

 829: function Detail({ label, value }) {
 830:   if (value === undefined || value === null || value === "") {
 831:     return null;
 832:   }
 833: 
 834:   return (
 835:     <div className="training-item-detail">
 836:       <span>{label}</span>
 837:       <strong>{value}</strong>
 838:     </div>
 839:   );
 840: }
 841: 
 842: function SectionTitle({ title, count }) {
 843:   return (
 844:     <header className="training-item-section-title">
 845:       <h4>{title}</h4>
 846: 
 847:       {count !== undefined && <span>{count}</span>}
 848:     </header>
 849:   );
 850: }
 851: 
 852: function OrderedDetails({
 853:   title,
 854:   items,
 855:   getTitle,
 856:   getDescription = () => "",
 857: }) {
 858:   return (
 859:     <section className="training-item-detail-section">
 860:       <SectionTitle title={title} count={items.length} />
 861: 
 862:       <div className="training-item-ordered-list">
 863:         {items.map((item, index) => (
 864:           <article key={item.id}>
 865:             <span>{String(index + 1).padStart(2, "0")}</span>
 866: 
 867:             <div>
 868:               <strong>{getTitle(item)}</strong>
 869: 
 870:               {getDescription(item) && <p>{getDescription(item)}</p>}
 871:             </div>
 872:           </article>
 873:         ))}
 874:       </div>
 875:     </section>
 876:   );

  65:   const [editorError, setEditorError] = useState("");
  66: 
  67:   /*
  68:    * Reset the draft if the editor configuration changes.
  69:    */
  70: 
  71:   useEffect(() => {
  72:     setDraft(createEmptyDraft(fields));
  73:     setEditingId(null);
  74:     setEditorError("");
  75:   }, [fields]);
  76: 
  77:   const orderedItems = useMemo(
  78:     () =>
  79:       [...values].sort(
  80:         (first, second) => (first.order ?? 0) - (second.order ?? 0),
  81:       ),
  82:     [values],
  83:   );
  84: 
  85:   const isEditing = Boolean(editingId);
  86: 
  87:   const hasReachedMaximum = values.length >= maximumItems;
  88: 
  89:   /*
  90:    * =========================================
  91:    * Draft Management
  92:    * =========================================
  93:    */
  94: 
  95:   const resetEditor = () => {
  96:     setDraft(createEmptyDraft(fields));
  97:     setEditingId(null);
  98:     setEditorError("");
  99:   };
 100: 
 101:   const updateDraft = (fieldName, value) => {
 102:     setDraft((current) => ({
 103:       ...current,
 104:       [fieldName]: value,
 105:     }));
 106: 
 107:     setEditorError("");
 108:   };
 109: 
 110:   /*
 111:    * =========================================
 112:    * Duplicate Detection
 113:    * =========================================
 114:    */
 115: 
 116:   const isDuplicate = (primaryValue) => {
 117:     const normalizedValue = getText(primaryValue)
 118:       .normalize("NFKC")
 119:       .toLocaleLowerCase();
 120: 
 121:     if (!normalizedValue) {
 122:       return false;
 123:     }
 124: 
 125:     return values.some((item) => {
 126:       if (item.id === editingId) {
 127:         return false;
 128:       }
 129: 
 130:       return (
 131:         getText(item?.[primaryField]).normalize("NFKC").toLocaleLowerCase() ===
 132:         normalizedValue
 133:       );
 134:     });
 135:   };
 136: 
 137:   /*
 138:    * =========================================
 139:    * Draft Validation
 140:    * =========================================
 141:    */
 142: 
 143:   const validateDraft = () => {
 144:     for (const field of fields) {
 145:       const value = getText(draft[field.name]);
 146: 
 147:       if (field.required !== false && !value) {
 148:         return `Enter ${field.label.toLowerCase()}.`;
 149:       }
 150: 
 151:       if (field.maximumLength && value.length > field.maximumLength) {
 152:         return `${field.label} cannot exceed ${field.maximumLength} characters.`;
 153:       }
 154:     }
 155: 
 156:     const primaryValue = getText(draft[primaryField]);
 157: 
 158:     if (!primaryValue) {
 159:       const primaryConfiguration = fields.find(
 160:         (field) => field.name === primaryField,
 161:       );
 162: 
 163:       return `Enter ${(
 164:         primaryConfiguration?.label || itemLabel
 165:       ).toLowerCase()}.`;
 166:     }
 167: 
 168:     if (isDuplicate(primaryValue)) {
 169:       return `This ${itemLabel.toLowerCase()} has already been added.`;
 170:     }
 171: 
 172:     if (!isEditing && hasReachedMaximum) {
 173:       return `Add no more than ${maximumItems} ${itemLabel.toLowerCase()} records.`;
 174:     }
 175: 
 176:     return "";
 177:   };
 178: 
 179:   /*
 180:    * =========================================
 181:    * Save
 182:    * =========================================
 183:    */
 184: 
 185:   const handleSave = () => {
 186:     const validationError = validateDraft();
 187: 
 188:     if (validationError) {
 189:       setEditorError(validationError);
 190:       return;
 191:     }
 192: 
 193:     const normalizedDraft = fields.reduce((result, field) => {
 194:       result[field.name] = getText(draft[field.name]);
 195: 
 196:       return result;
 197:     }, {});

 199:     if (isEditing) {

 201:         normalizeOrder(

 203:             item.id === editingId
 204:               ? {
 205:                   ...item,
 206:                   ...normalizedDraft,
 207:                 }
 208:               : item,
 209:           ),
 210:         ),
 211:       );
 212:     } else {
 213:       if (typeof createItem !== "function") {
 214:         setEditorError(
 215:           `The ${itemLabel.toLowerCase()} creation function is unavailable.`,
 216:         );
 217: 
 218:         return;
 219:       }
 220: 
 221:       const createdItem = createItem({
 222:         ...normalizedDraft,
 223:         order: values.length,
 224:       });
 225: 
 226:       onChange?.(normalizeOrder([...values, createdItem]));
 227:     }
 228: 
 229:     resetEditor();
 230:   };
 231: 
 232:   /*
 233:    * =========================================
 234:    * Edit
 235:    * =========================================
 236:    */
 237: 
 238:   const handleEdit = (item) => {
 239:     const nextDraft = fields.reduce((result, field) => {
 240:       result[field.name] =
 241:         typeof item?.[field.name] === "string" ? item[field.name] : "";
 242: 
 243:       return result;
 244:     }, {});
 245: 
 246:     setDraft(nextDraft);
 247:     setEditingId(item.id);
 248:     setEditorError("");
 249:   };
 250: 
 251:   /*
 252:    * =========================================
 253:    * Remove
 254:    * =========================================
 255:    */
 256: 
 257:   const handleRemove = (itemId) => {
 258:     onChange?.(normalizeOrder(values.filter((item) => item.id !== itemId)));
 259: 
 260:     if (editingId === itemId) {
 261:       resetEditor();
 262:     }
 263:   };
 264: 
 265:   /*
 266:    * =========================================
 267:    * Reorder
 268:    * =========================================
 269:    */
 270: 
 271:   const handleMove = (itemId, direction) => {
 272:     const currentIndex = orderedItems.findIndex((item) => item.id === itemId);
 273: 
 274:     if (currentIndex === -1) {
 275:       return;
 276:     }
 277: 
 278:     const targetIndex =
 279:       direction === "up" ? currentIndex - 1 : currentIndex + 1;
 280: 
 281:     if (targetIndex < 0 || targetIndex >= orderedItems.length) {
 282:       return;
 283:     }
 284: 
 285:     const reorderedItems = [...orderedItems];
 286: 
 287:     const [movedItem] = reorderedItems.splice(currentIndex, 1);
 288: 
 289:     reorderedItems.splice(targetIndex, 0, movedItem);
 290: 
 291:     onChange?.(normalizeOrder(reorderedItems));
 292:   };
 293: 
 294:   /*
 295:    * =========================================
 296:    * Render
 297:    * =========================================
 298:    */
 299: 
 300:   return (
 301:     <section
 302:       className="training-form-section training-ordered-editor"
 303:       aria-labelledby={titleId}
 304:     >
 305:       <header className="training-form-section-header">
 306:         <span aria-hidden="true" />
 307: 
 308:         <div>
 309:           <small>{label}</small>
 310: 
 311:           <h3 id={titleId}>{title}</h3>
 312: 
 313:           <p>{description}</p>
 314:         </div>
 315:       </header>
 316: 
 317:       <div className="training-ordered-editor-form">
 318:         {fields.map((field) => {
 319:           const value = draft[field.name] || "";
 320: 
 321:           return (
 322:             <div
 323:               className={`training-ordered-editor-field ${
 324:                 field.wide ? "training-ordered-editor-wide" : ""
 325:               }`}
 326:               key={field.name}
 327:             >
 328:               <div className="training-ordered-editor-label">
 329:                 <label htmlFor={`${titleId}-${field.name}`}>
 330:                   {field.label}
 331: 
 332:                   {field.required !== false ? (
 333:                     <small> Required</small>
 334:                   ) : (
 335:                     <small> Optional</small>
 336:                   )}
 337:                 </label>
 338: 
 339:                 {field.maximumLength && (
 340:                   <span>
 341:                     {value.length}/{field.maximumLength}
 342:                   </span>
 343:                 )}
 344:               </div>
 345: 
 346:               {field.multiline ? (
 347:                 <textarea
 348:                   id={`${titleId}-${field.name}`}
 349:                   rows={field.rows || 3}
 350:                   value={value}
 351:                   onChange={(event) =>
 352:                     updateDraft(field.name, event.target.value)
 353:                   }
 354:                   maxLength={field.maximumLength}
 355:                   placeholder={field.placeholder}
 356:                   disabled={disabled}
 357:                 />
 358:               ) : (
 359:                 <input
 360:                   id={`${titleId}-${field.name}`}
 361:                   type="text"
 362:                   value={value}
 363:                   onChange={(event) =>
 364:                     updateDraft(field.name, event.target.value)
 365:                   }
 366:                   maxLength={field.maximumLength}
 367:                   placeholder={field.placeholder}
 368:                   disabled={disabled}
 369:                 />
 370:               )}
 371:             </div>
 372:           );
 373:         })}
 374: 
 375:         {editorError && (
 376:           <p
 377:             className="training-ordered-editor-error training-ordered-editor-wide"
 378:             role="alert"
 379:           >
 380:             {editorError}
 381:           </p>
 382:         )}
 383: 
 384:         <footer className="training-ordered-editor-form-footer training-ordered-editor-wide">
 385:           <small>
 386:             {values.length}/{maximumItems}{" "}
 387:             {values.length === 1
 388:               ? itemLabel.toLowerCase()
 389:               : `${itemLabel.toLowerCase()} records`}
 390:           </small>
 391: 
 392:           <div>
 393:             {isEditing && (
 394:               <button type="button" onClick={resetEditor} disabled={disabled}>
 395:                 Cancel Edit
 396:               </button>

 398: 
 399:             <button
 400:               type="button"
 401:               className="training-ordered-editor-add"
 402:               onClick={handleSave}
 403:               disabled={disabled || (!isEditing && hasReachedMaximum)}
 404:             >
 405:               {isEditing ? `Save ${itemLabel}` : `+ Add ${itemLabel}`}
 406:             </button>
 407:           </div>
 408:         </footer>
 409:       </div>
 410: 
 411:       {fieldErrors[fieldPath] && (
 412:         <p className="training-ordered-editor-error" role="alert">
 413:           {fieldErrors[fieldPath]}
 414:         </p>
 415:       )}
 416: 
 417:       {orderedItems.length > 0 ? (
 418:         <div className="training-ordered-editor-list">
 419:           {orderedItems.map((item, index) => {
 420:             const itemErrors = fields
 421:               .map(
 422:                 (field) => fieldErrors[`${fieldPath}.${index}.${field.name}`],

 424:               .filter(Boolean);

 447:                       â†‘
 448:                     </button>

  15:  * =========================================
  16:  * Order Normalization
  17:  * =========================================
  18:  */
  19: 
  20: function normalizeOrder(relationships) {
  21:   return relationships.map((relationship, index) => ({
  22:     ...relationship,
  23:     order: index,
  24:   }));
  25: }
  26: 
  27: /*
  28:  * =========================================
  29:  * Training Skills Editor

  31:  */

  33: function TrainingSkillsEditor({
  34:   skillRelationships = [],

  36:   disabled = false,
  37:   onChange,
  38: }) {

 265: 
 266:             const relationshipError =
 267:               fieldErrors[`skillRelationships.${index}.skillId`] ||
 268:               fieldErrors[`skillRelationships.${index}.nameSnapshot`];
 269: 
 270:             return (
 271:               <article
 272:                 key={relationship.id}
 273:                 className={`training-skills-editor-item ${
 274:                   relationshipError ? "training-skills-editor-item--error" : ""
 275:                 }`}
 276:               >
 277:                 <div className="training-skills-editor-order">
 278:                   <span>{String(index + 1).padStart(2, "0")}</span>
 279: 
 280:                   <div>
 281:                     <button
 282:                       type="button"
 283:                       onClick={() => handleMove(relationship.id, "up")}
 284:                       disabled={isWorking || index === 0}
 285:                       aria-label={`Move ${skillName} up`}
 286:                     >
 287:                       â†‘
 288:                     </button>
 289: 
 290:                     <button
 291:                       type="button"
 292:                       onClick={() => handleMove(relationship.id, "down")}
 293:                       disabled={
 294:                         isWorking || index === orderedRelationships.length - 1
 295:                       }
 296:                       aria-label={`Move ${skillName} down`}
 297:                     >
 298:                       â†“
 299:                     </button>
 300:                   </div>
 301:                 </div>
 302: 
 303:                 <div className="training-skills-editor-content">
 304:                   <div className="training-skills-editor-labels">
 305:                     <span>{getSkillCategoryLabel(category)}</span>
 306: 
 307:                     <span>{type}</span>
 308:                   </div>
 309: 
 310:                   <strong>{skillName}</strong>
 311: 
 312:                   {!librarySkill && (
 313:                     <small className="training-skills-editor-warning">
 314:                       Library record unavailable; saved snapshot will be used.
 315:                     </small>
 316:                   )}
 317: 
 318:                   {relationshipError && (
 319:                     <small
 320:                       className="training-skills-editor-field-error"
 321:                       role="alert"
 322:                     >
 323:                       {relationshipError}
 324:                     </small>
 325:                   )}
 326:                 </div>
 327: 
 328:                 <button
 329:                   type="button"
 330:                   className="training-skills-editor-remove"
 331:                   onClick={() => handleRemove(relationship.id)}
 332:                   disabled={isWorking}
 333:                   aria-label={`Remove ${skillName} from this training`}
 334:                 >
 335:                   Remove
 336:                 </button>
 337:               </article>
 338:             );
 339:           })}
 340:         </div>
 341:       ) : (
 342:         <div className="training-skills-editor-empty">
 343:           <span aria-hidden="true">âœ¦</span>
 344: 
 345:           <h4>No skills selected</h4>
 346: 
 347:           <p>Add skills developed or practiced through this training.</p>
 348:         </div>
 349:       )}
 350:     </section>
 351:   );
 352: }
 353: 
 354: export default TrainingSkillsEditor;

  53:   return typeof value === "string" ? value.trim() : "";
  54: }

  57:   return Array.isArray(value) ? value : [];

  61:   return getText(value).normalize("NFKC").toLocaleLowerCase();

  74: function getBrowserStorage() {
  75:   if (typeof window === "undefined" || !window.localStorage) {
  76:     const error = new Error(
  77:       "Migration storage is unavailable in this environment.",
  78:     );
  79: 
  80:     error.name = "CertificationTrainingMigrationError";
  81:     error.code = "CERTIFICATION_TRAINING_MIGRATION_STORAGE_UNAVAILABLE";
  82:     error.publicMessage =
  83:       "Certification migration storage is not available in this browser.";
  84: 
  85:     throw error;
  86:   }
  87: 
  88:   return window.localStorage;
  89: }

  91: function readMigrationState() {
  92:   const storage = getBrowserStorage();
  93: 
  94:   const storedValue = storage.getItem(CERTIFICATION_TRAINING_MIGRATION_KEY);
  95: 
  96:   if (!storedValue) {
  97:     return null;
  98:   }
  99: 
 100:   try {
 101:     const parsedValue = JSON.parse(storedValue);
 102: 
 103:     return parsedValue &&
 104:       typeof parsedValue === "object" &&
 105:       !Array.isArray(parsedValue)
 106:       ? parsedValue
 107:       : null;
 108:   } catch {
 109:     /*
 110:      * An invalid marker should not permanently
 111:      * prevent the migration from running.
 112:      */
 113: 
 114:     return null;
 115:   }
 116: }

 118: function writeMigrationState(report) {
 119:   const storage = getBrowserStorage();
 120: 
 121:   const state = {
 122:     version: CERTIFICATION_TRAINING_MIGRATION_VERSION,
 123:     completedAt: new Date().toISOString(),
 124: 
 125:     summary: {
 126:       examined: report.examined,
 127:       eligible: report.eligible,
 128:       created: report.created.length,
 129:       linked: report.linked.length,
 130:       skipped: report.skipped.length,
 131:       documentCopies: report.documentCopies.length,
 132:       documentFailures: report.documentFailures.length,
 133:       failures: report.failures.length,
 134:     },
 135:   };
 136: 
 137:   storage.setItem(CERTIFICATION_TRAINING_MIGRATION_KEY, JSON.stringify(state));
 138: 
 139:   return state;
 140: }

 142: export function getCertificationTrainingMigrationState() {
 143:   return readMigrationState();

 156: function createMigrationError({
 157:   message,
 158:   publicMessage,
 159:   code = "CERTIFICATION_TRAINING_MIGRATION_FAILED",
 160:   details = null,
 161:   cause = null,
 162: }) {
 163:   const error = new Error(message);
 164: 
 165:   error.name = "CertificationTrainingMigrationError";
 166:   error.code = code;
 167:   error.status = 500;
 168:   error.publicMessage = publicMessage;
 169:   error.details = details;
 170: 
 171:   if (cause) {
 172:     error.cause = cause;
 173:   }
 174: 
 175:   return error;

 180:  * Eligibility

 184: function isCertificateDocument(document) {
 185:   const documentType = normalizeComparisonText(document?.documentType);
 186: 
 187:   const searchableText = [
 188:     document?.name,
 189:     document?.fileName,
 190:     document?.description,
 191:   ]
 192:     .map(normalizeComparisonText)
 193:     .filter(Boolean)
 194:     .join(" ");
 195: 
 196:   return (
 197:     documentType.includes("certificate") ||
 198:     documentType.includes("credential") ||
 199:     searchableText.includes("certificate") ||
 200:     searchableText.includes("credential")
 201:   );
 202: }
 203: 
 204: function getLegacyCertificateDocuments(training) {
 205:   const documents = getArray(training?.supportingDocuments);
 206: 
 207:   const certificateDocuments = documents.filter(isCertificateDocument);
 208: 
 209:   if (certificateDocuments.length > 0) {
 210:     return certificateDocuments.slice(
 211:       0,
 212:       CERTIFICATION_FIELD_LIMITS.maximumSupportingDocuments,
 213:     );
 214:   }
 215: 
 216:   /*
 217:    * Older Training records may not have used a
 218:    * certificate-specific document type.
 219:    *
 220:    * If a certificate was marked as earned and only
 221:    * one document exists, it is a reasonable migration
 222:    * candidate.
 223:    */
 224: 
 225:   if (
 226:     training?.completion?.certificateEarned === true &&
 227:     documents.length === 1
 228:   ) {
 229:     return documents;
 230:   }
 231: 
 232:   return [];
 233: }
 234: 
 235: function hasLegacyCertificateInformation(training) {
 236:   return Boolean(
 237:     training?.completion?.certificateEarned === true ||
 238:     getText(training?.completion?.credentialId) ||
 239:     getText(training?.completion?.credentialUrl) ||
 240:     getLegacyCertificateDocuments(training).length > 0,
 241:   );
 242: }

 244: /*
 245:  * =========================================
 246:  * Matching Existing Certifications
 247:  * =========================================
 248:  */
 249: 
 250: function getCertificationCredentialId(certification) {
 251:   return normalizeComparisonText(certification?.credential?.credentialId);
 252: }
 253: 
 254: function getCertificationVerificationUrl(certification) {
 255:   return normalizeComparisonText(certification?.credential?.verificationUrl);
 256: }
 257: 
 258: function findMatchingCertification(certifications, training) {
 259:   const trainingCredentialId = normalizeComparisonText(
 260:     training?.completion?.credentialId,
 261:   );
 262: 
 263:   const trainingCredentialUrl = normalizeComparisonText(
 264:     training?.completion?.credentialUrl,
 265:   );
 266: 
 267:   /*
 268:    * Credential ID is the strongest match.
 269:    */
 270: 
 271:   if (trainingCredentialId) {
 272:     const credentialMatch = certifications.find(
 273:       (certification) =>
 274:         getCertificationCredentialId(certification) === trainingCredentialId,
 275:     );
 276: 
 277:     if (credentialMatch) {
 278:       return credentialMatch;
 279:     }
 280:   }
 281: 
 282:   /*
 283:    * Verification URL is the next strongest match.
 284:    */
 285: 
 286:   if (trainingCredentialUrl) {
 287:     const urlMatch = certifications.find(
 288:       (certification) =>
 289:         getCertificationVerificationUrl(certification) ===
 290:         trainingCredentialUrl,
 291:     );
 292: 
 293:     if (urlMatch) {
 294:       return urlMatch;
 295:     }
 296:   }
 297: 
 298:   /*
 299:    * Fall back to Training title and provider name.
 300:    */
 301: 
 302:   const trainingTitle = normalizeComparisonText(training?.title);
 303: 
 304:   const providerName = normalizeComparisonText(training?.provider?.name);
 305: 
 306:   if (!trainingTitle || !providerName) {
 307:     return null;
 308:   }
 309: 
 310:   return (
 311:     certifications.find(
 312:       (certification) =>
 313:         normalizeComparisonText(certification?.name) === trainingTitle &&
 314:         normalizeComparisonText(certification?.issuingOrganization?.name) ===
 315:           providerName,
 316:     ) || null
 317:   );
 318: }

 326: function getCertificationTrainingIds(certification) {
 327:   return uniqueTextValues(
 328:     getArray(certification?.relatedRecords?.trainingRelationships).map(
 329:       (relationship) =>
 330:         relationship?.trainingId || relationship?.trainingRecordId,
 331:     ),
 332:   );
 333: }

 335: function addTrainingRelationship(certification, training) {
 336:   const currentRelationships = getArray(
 337:     certification?.relatedRecords?.trainingRelationships,
 338:   );
 339: 
 340:   const alreadyLinked = currentRelationships.some(
 341:     (relationship) =>
 342:       getText(relationship?.trainingId || relationship?.trainingRecordId) ===
 343:       training.id,
 344:   );
 345: 
 346:   if (alreadyLinked) {
 347:     return certification;
 348:   }
 349: 
 350:   if (
 351:     currentRelationships.length >=
 352:     CERTIFICATION_FIELD_LIMITS.maximumRelatedTrainingRecords
 353:   ) {
 354:     throw createMigrationError({
 355:       message:
 356:         `Certification "${certification.id}" cannot accept ` +
 357:         `Training relationship "${training.id}".`,
 358: 
 359:       publicMessage:
 360:         "A Certification reached its maximum number of linked Training records.",
 361: 
 362:       code: "CERTIFICATION_TRAINING_MIGRATION_RELATIONSHIP_LIMIT",
 363: 
 364:       details: {
 365:         certificationId: certification.id,
 366:         trainingId: training.id,
 367:       },
 368:     });
 369:   }
 370: 
 371:   return normalizeCertification({
 372:     ...certification,
 373: 
 374:     relatedRecords: {
 375:       ...certification.relatedRecords,
 376: 
 377:       trainingRelationships: [
 378:         ...currentRelationships,
 379: 
 380:         createCertificationTrainingRelationship(
 381:           training,
 382:           currentRelationships.length,
 383:         ),
 384:       ],
 385:     },
 386: 
 387:     updatedAt: new Date().toISOString(),
 388:   });
 389: }

 408:   }

 418: function createCertificationFromTraining(training) {
 419:   return createCertificationModel({
 420:     name: getText(training?.title) || "Training Certification",
 421: 
 422:     certificationType: "professional",
 423: 
 424:     issuingOrganization: {
 425:       name: getText(training?.provider?.name) || "Training Provider",
 426: 
 427:       type: "training-provider",
 428: 
 429:       website: getText(training?.provider?.website),
 430:     },
 431: 
 432:     dates: {
 433:       issueDate: getCertificationIssueDate(training),
 434:       expirationDate: "",
 435:       lastRenewedDate: "",
 436:       nextRenewalDate: "",
 437:       doesNotExpire: true,
 438:     },
 439: 
 440:     credential: {
 441:       state: "active",
 442: 
 443:       credentialId: getText(training?.completion?.credentialId),
 444: 
 445:       verificationUrl: getText(training?.completion?.credentialUrl),
 446:     },
 447: 
 448:     description: getCertificationDescription(training),
 449: 
 450:     skillRelationships: [],
 451: 
 452:     relatedRecords: {
 453:       trainingRelationships: [
 454:         createCertificationTrainingRelationship(training, 0),
 455:       ],
 456: 
 457:       educationIds: [],
 458:     },
 459: 
 460:     supportingDocuments: [],
 461: 
 462:     visibility: {
 463:       showIssuingOrganization: true,
 464:       showIssuerWebsite: Boolean(getText(training?.provider?.website)),
 465:       showDates: true,
 466:       showExpirationDate: true,
 467:       showCredentialId: Boolean(getText(training?.completion?.credentialId)),
 468:       showVerificationUrl: Boolean(
 469:         getText(training?.completion?.credentialUrl),
 470:       ),
 471:       showDescription: true,
 472:       showSkills: true,
 473:       showSupportingDocuments: false,
 474:     },
 475: 
 476:     privateInformation: {
 477:       notes: "",
 478:     },
 479: 
 480:     source: "training",
 481: 
 482:     sourceContext: `Migrated from Training "${getText(
 483:       training?.title,
 484:     )}" (${training.id}).`.slice(0, CERTIFICATION_FIELD_LIMITS.sourceContext),
 485: 
 486:     status: training?.status === "archived" ? "archived" : "active",
 487:   });
 488: }

 490: /*
 491:  * =========================================
 492:  * File Conversion
 493:  * =========================================
 494:  */
 495: 
 496: function createFileFromStoredValue(storedFile, document) {
 497:   if (typeof File === "undefined") {
 498:     return null;
 499:   }
 500: 
 501:   if (storedFile instanceof File) {
 502:     return storedFile;
 503:   }
 504: 
 505:   if (typeof Blob !== "undefined" && storedFile instanceof Blob) {
 506:     return new File(
 507:       [storedFile],
 508:       getText(document?.fileName) ||
 509:         getText(document?.name) ||
 510:         "certificate-document",
 511:       {
 512:         type:
 513:           getText(document?.mimeType) ||
 514:           storedFile.type ||
 515:           "application/octet-stream",
 516: 
 517:         lastModified: Date.now(),
 518:       },
 519:     );
 520:   }
 521: 
 522:   return null;
 523: }
 524: 
 525: async function copyTrainingCertificateDocuments(
 526:   training,
 527:   certification,
 528:   report,
 529: ) {
 530:   const copiedDocuments = [];
 531:   const copiedStorageKeys = [];
 532: 
 533:   const legacyDocuments = getLegacyCertificateDocuments(training);
 534: 
 535:   for (const legacyDocument of legacyDocuments) {
 536:     const originalStorageKey = getText(legacyDocument?.storageKey);
 537: 
 538:     if (!originalStorageKey) {
 539:       report.documentFailures.push({
 540:         trainingId: training.id,
 541:         certificationId: certification.id,
 542:         documentId: getText(legacyDocument?.id),
 543:         fileName: getText(legacyDocument?.fileName),
 544:         reason: "The Training document has no storage key.",
 545:       });
 546: 
 547:       continue;
 548:     }
 549: 
 550:     try {
 551:       const storedValue = await getStoredDocumentFile(originalStorageKey);
 552: 
 553:       const file = createFileFromStoredValue(storedValue, legacyDocument);
 554: 
 555:       if (!file) {
 556:         throw new Error("The Training document file is unavailable.");
 557:       }
 558: 
 559:       if (!isSupportedCertificationDocument(file)) {
 560:         throw new Error(
 561:           "The Training document format is not supported by Certification storage.",
 562:         );
 563:       }
 564: 
 565:       if (!isCertificationDocumentWithinSizeLimit(file)) {
 566:         throw new Error(
 567:           "The Training document exceeds the Certification file-size limit.",
 568:         );
 569:       }
 570: 
 571:       const storedDocument = await writeCertificationDocument({
 572:         file,
 573:         certificationId: certification.id,
 574:       });
 575: 
 576:       copiedStorageKeys.push(storedDocument.storageKey);
 577: 
 578:       const copiedDocument = createCertificationDocument(
 579:         {
 580:           id: storedDocument.documentId,
 581: 
 582:           documentType: "certificate",
 583: 
 584:           name:
 585:             getText(legacyDocument?.name) ||
 586:             getText(certification?.name) ||
 587:             storedDocument.fileName,
 588: 
 589:           description:
 590:             getText(legacyDocument?.description) ||
 591:             "Certificate migrated from the Training Library.",
 592: 
 593:           fileName: storedDocument.fileName,
 594:           extension: storedDocument.extension,
 595:           mimeType: storedDocument.mimeType,
 596:           size: storedDocument.size,
 597:           storageKey: storedDocument.storageKey,
 598:           previewMode: storedDocument.previewMode,
 599: 
 600:           isPrimary: copiedDocuments.length === 0,
 601: 
 602:           status: "active",
 603: 
 604:           uploadedAt:
 605:             getText(legacyDocument?.uploadedAt) || storedDocument.updatedAt,
 606: 
 607:           createdAt: storedDocument.createdAt,
 608:           updatedAt: storedDocument.updatedAt,
 609:         },
 610:         copiedDocuments.length,
 611:       );
 612: 
 613:       copiedDocuments.push(copiedDocument);
 614: 
 615:       report.documentCopies.push({
 616:         trainingId: training.id,
 617:         certificationId: certification.id,
 618:         sourceDocumentId: getText(legacyDocument?.id),
 619:         sourceStorageKey: originalStorageKey,
 620:         certificationStorageKey: storedDocument.storageKey,
 621:         fileName: storedDocument.fileName,
 622:       });
 623:     } catch (error) {
 624:       report.documentFailures.push({
 625:         trainingId: training.id,
 626:         certificationId: certification.id,
 627:         documentId: getText(legacyDocument?.id),
 628:         fileName: getText(legacyDocument?.fileName),
 629:         reason:
 630:           error?.publicMessage ||
 631:           error?.message ||
 632:           "The document could not be migrated.",
 633:       });
 634:     }
 635:   }
 636: 
 637:   return {
 638:     certification: normalizeCertification({
 639:       ...certification,
 640: 
 641:       supportingDocuments: copiedDocuments,
 642: 
 643:       visibility: {
 644:         ...certification.visibility,
 645: 
 646:         showSupportingDocuments: copiedDocuments.length > 0,
 647:       },
 648:     }),
 649: 
 650:     copiedStorageKeys,
 651:   };
 652: }
 653: 
 654: /*
 655:  * =========================================
 656:  * Report
 657:  * =========================================
 658:  */
 659: 
 660: function createMigrationReport() {
 661:   return {
 662:     version: CERTIFICATION_TRAINING_MIGRATION_VERSION,
 663: 
 664:     alreadyCompleted: false,
 665: 
 666:     examined: 0,
 667: 
 668:     eligible: 0,
 669: 
 670:     created: [],
 671: 
 672:     linked: [],
 673: 
 674:     skipped: [],
 675: 
 676:     documentCopies: [],
 677: 
 678:     documentFailures: [],
 679: 
 680:     failures: [],
 681: 
 682:     completedAt: "",
 683:   };
 684: }
 685: 
 686: /*
 687:  * =========================================
 688:  * Main Migration
 689:  * =========================================
 690:  */
 691: 
 692: export async function migrateLegacyTrainingCertifications({
 693:   force = false,
 694: } = {}) {
 695:   const previousState = readMigrationState();
 696: 
 697:   if (
 698:     !force &&
 699:     Number(previousState?.version) >= CERTIFICATION_TRAINING_MIGRATION_VERSION
 700:   ) {
 701:     return {
 702:       ...createMigrationReport(),
 703: 
 704:       alreadyCompleted: true,
 705: 
 706:       completedAt: getText(previousState?.completedAt),
 707: 
 708:       previousState,
 709:     };
 710:   }
 711: 
 712:   const report = createMigrationReport();
 713: 
 714:   const originalTrainingRecords =
 715:     normalizeTrainingCollection(readStoredTraining());
 716: 
 717:   const originalCertificationRecords = normalizeCertificationCollection(
 718:     readStoredCertifications(),
 719:   );
 720: 
 721:   report.examined = originalTrainingRecords.length;
 722: 
 723:   const eligibleTrainingRecords = originalTrainingRecords.filter(
 724:     hasLegacyCertificateInformation,
 725:   );
 726: 
 727:   report.eligible = eligibleTrainingRecords.length;
 728: 
 729:   let nextCertificationRecords = [...originalCertificationRecords];
 730: 
 731:   const changedCertificationIds = new Set();
 732: 
 733:   const copiedStorageKeys = [];
 734: 
 735:   try {
 736:     for (const training of eligibleTrainingRecords) {
 737:       const recordCopiedStorageKeyStart = copiedStorageKeys.length;
 738:       try {
 739:         const linkedCertificationIds = getTrainingCertificationIds(training);
 740: 
 741:         /*
 742:          * This Training has already been migrated or
 743:          * manually linked. Do not create another record.
 744:          */
 745: 
 746:         if (linkedCertificationIds.length > 0) {
 747:           report.skipped.push({
 748:             trainingId: training.id,
 749:             reason: "Training already has a Certification relationship.",
 750:             certificationIds: linkedCertificationIds,
 751:           });
 752: 
 753:           continue;
 754:         }
 755: 
 756:         let certification = findMatchingCertification(
 757:           nextCertificationRecords,
 758:           training,
 759:         );
 760: 
 761:         if (certification) {
 762:           certification = addTrainingRelationship(certification, training);
 763: 
 764:           nextCertificationRecords = nextCertificationRecords.map((record) =>
 765:             record.id === certification.id ? certification : record,
 766:           );
 767: 
 768:           changedCertificationIds.add(certification.id);
 769: 
 770:           report.linked.push({
 771:             trainingId: training.id,
 772:             certificationId: certification.id,
 773:             created: false,
 774:           });
 775: 
 776:           continue;
 777:         }
 778: 
 779:         certification = createCertificationFromTraining(training);
 780: 
 781:         const copied = await copyTrainingCertificateDocuments(
 782:           training,
 783:           certification,
 784:           report,
 785:         );
 786: 
 787:         certification = copied.certification;
 788: 
 789:         copiedStorageKeys.push(...copied.copiedStorageKeys);
 790: 
 791:         const validation = validateCertification(certification, {
 792:           trainingRecords: originalTrainingRecords,
 793:         });
 794: 
 795:         if (!validation.isValid) {
 796:           throw createMigrationError({
 797:             message:
 798:               `Migrated Certification for Training ` +
 799:               `"${training.id}" did not pass validation.`,
 800: 
 801:             publicMessage:
 802:               "A legacy Training certificate could not be converted.",
 803: 
 804:             code: "CERTIFICATION_TRAINING_MIGRATION_VALIDATION_FAILED",
 805: 
 806:             details: {
 807:               trainingId: training.id,
 808:               validation,
 809:             },
 810:           });
 811:         }
 812: 
 813:         certification = validation.certification;
 814: 
 815:         nextCertificationRecords.push(certification);
 816: 
 817:         changedCertificationIds.add(certification.id);
 818: 
 819:         report.created.push({
 820:           trainingId: training.id,
 821:           certificationId: certification.id,
 822:           name: certification.name,
 823:         });
 824: 
 825:         report.linked.push({
 826:           trainingId: training.id,
 827:           certificationId: certification.id,
 828:           created: true,
 829:         });
 830:       } catch (recordError) {
 831:         report.failures.push({
 832:           trainingId: training.id,
 833: 
 834:           message:
 835:             recordError?.publicMessage ||
 836:             recordError?.message ||
 837:             "The Training certificate could not be migrated.",
 838: 
 839:           code:
 840:             recordError?.code ||
 841:             "CERTIFICATION_TRAINING_RECORD_MIGRATION_FAILED",
 842:         });
 843:       }
 844:     }
 845: 
 846:     /*
 847:      * Even if no new Certification was needed, write the
 848:      * migration marker so the scan is not repeated.
 849:      */
 850: 
 851:     if (changedCertificationIds.size > 0) {
 852:       saveCertificationWithTrainingSync({
 853:         certificationRecords: normalizeCertificationCollection(
 854:           nextCertificationRecords,
 855:         ),
 856: 
 857:         changedCertificationIds: [...changedCertificationIds],
 858:       });
 859:     }
 860: 
 861:     report.completedAt = new Date().toISOString();
 862: 
 863:     writeMigrationState(report);
 864: 
 865:     return report;
 866:   } catch (recordError) {
 867:     const recordStorageKeys = copiedStorageKeys.splice(
 868:       recordCopiedStorageKeyStart,
 869:     );
 870: 
 871:     if (recordStorageKeys.length > 0) {
 872:       await deleteCertificationDocuments(recordStorageKeys).catch(() => {});
 873:     }
 874: 
 875:     report.failures.push({
 876:       trainingId: training.id,
 877: 
 878:       message:
 879:         recordError?.publicMessage ||
 880:         recordError?.message ||
 881:         "The Training certificate could not be migrated.",
 882: 
 883:       code:
 884:         recordError?.code || "CERTIFICATION_TRAINING_RECORD_MIGRATION_FAILED",
 885:     });
 886:   }
 887: }

 126: function requireTraining(trainingMap, trainingId) {
 127:   const training = trainingMap.get(trainingId);
 128: 
 129:   if (!training) {
 130:     throw createRelationshipError(
 131:       "TRAINING_RELATIONSHIP_NOT_FOUND",
 132:       `Training record "${trainingId}" could not be found during relationship synchronization.`,
 133:       "A linked Training record could not be found.",
 134:       {
 135:         trainingId,
 136:       },
 137:     );
 138:   }
 139: 
 140:   return training;

 148:       "CERTIFICATION_RELATIONSHIP_NOT_FOUND",
 149:       `Certification record "${certificationId}" could not be found during relationship synchronization.`,
 150:       "A linked Certification record could not be found.",
 151:       {
 152:         certificationId,
 153:       },
 154:     );
 155:   }
 156:

 186:       (relationship) => relationship.certificationId,
 187:     ),
 188: 
 189:     updatedAt: new Date().toISOString(),
 190:   };

 196:   const trainingRelationships = trainingIds.map((trainingId, index) => {

 198:

 214: /*
 215:  * =========================================
 216:  * Add or Remove One Relationship
 217:  * =========================================
 218:  */
 219: 
 220: function synchronizeCertificationWithTraining(
 221:   certification,
 222:   training,
 223:   shouldBeLinked,
 224: ) {
 225:   const currentRelationships = getArray(
 226:     certification?.relatedRecords?.trainingRelationships,
 227:   );
 228: 
 229:   const existingRelationship = currentRelationships.find(
 230:     (relationship) => getText(relationship?.trainingId) === training.id,
 231:   );
 232: 
 233:   /*
 234:    * The Certification is not linked and does not
 235:    * need to be linked. Preserve it unchanged.
 236:    */
 237: 
 238:   if (!shouldBeLinked && !existingRelationship) {
 239:     return certification;
 240:   }
 241: 
 242:   const nextRelationships = currentRelationships.filter(
 243:     (relationship) => getText(relationship?.trainingId) !== training.id,
 244:   );
 245: 
 246:   if (shouldBeLinked) {
 247:     nextRelationships.push(
 248:       createCertificationTrainingRelationship(
 249:         training,
 250:         nextRelationships.length,
 251:       ),
 252:     );
 253:   }
 254: 
 255:   return normalizeCertification({
 256:     ...certification,
 257: 
 258:     relatedRecords: {
 259:       ...certification.relatedRecords,
 260: 
 261:       trainingRelationships: nextRelationships,
 262:     },
 263: 
 264:     updatedAt: new Date().toISOString(),
 265:   });
 266: }
 267: 
 268: function synchronizeTrainingWithCertification(
 269:   training,
 270:   certification,
 271:   shouldBeLinked,
 272: ) {
 273:   const currentRelationships = getArray(training?.certificationRelationships);
 274: 
 275:   const existingRelationship = currentRelationships.find(
 276:     (relationship) =>
 277:       getText(relationship?.certificationId) === certification.id,
 278:   );
 279: 
 280:   /*
 281:    * This Training is not linked and does not need
 282:    * to be linked. Preserve the record unchanged.
 283:    */
 284: 
 285:   if (!shouldBeLinked && !existingRelationship) {
 286:     return training;
 287:   }
 288: 
 289:   const nextRelationships = currentRelationships.filter(
 290:     (relationship) =>
 291:       getText(relationship?.certificationId) !== certification.id,
 292:   );
 293: 
 294:   if (shouldBeLinked) {
 295:     nextRelationships.push(
 296:       createTrainingCertificationRelationship(
 297:         certification,
 298:         nextRelationships.length,
 299:       ),
 300:     );
 301:   }
 302: 
 303:   const certificationRelationships = nextRelationships.map(
 304:     (relationship, index) => ({
 305:       ...relationship,
 306:       order: index,
 307:     }),
 308:   );
 309: 
 310:   const certificationState = getText(certification?.credential?.state);
 311: 
 312:   const representsEarnedCertificate =
 313:     shouldBeLinked && ["active", "expired"].includes(certificationState);
 314: 
 315:   return {
 316:     ...training,
 317: 
 318:     certificationRelationships,
 319: 
 320:     relatedCertificationIds: certificationRelationships.map(
 321:       (relationship) => relationship.certificationId,
 322:     ),
 323: 
 324:     completion: {
 325:       ...training.completion,
 326: 
 327:       certificateEarned: representsEarnedCertificate
 328:         ? true
 329:         : training.completion?.certificateEarned === true,
 330:     },
 331: 
 332:     updatedAt: new Date().toISOString(),
 333:   };
 334: }
 335: 
 336: /*
 337:  * =========================================
 338:  * Application-Level Transaction
 339:  * =========================================
 340:  */
 341: 
 342: function commitCollections({
 343:   originalTrainingRecords,
 344:   originalCertificationRecords,
 345:   nextTrainingRecords,
 346:   nextCertificationRecords,
 347: }) {
 348:   let trainingWriteCompleted = false;
 349:   let certificationWriteCompleted = false;
 350: 
 351:   try {
 352:     const storedTrainingRecords = replaceStoredTraining(nextTrainingRecords);
 353: 
 354:     trainingWriteCompleted = true;
 355: 
 356:     const storedCertificationRecords = replaceStoredCertifications(
 357:       nextCertificationRecords,
 358:     );
 359: 
 360:     certificationWriteCompleted = true;
 361: 
 362:     return {
 363:       trainingRecords: storedTrainingRecords,
 364:       certificationRecords: storedCertificationRecords,
 365:     };
 366:   } catch (cause) {
 367:     const rollbackErrors = [];
 368: 
 369:     if (trainingWriteCompleted || certificationWriteCompleted) {
 370:       try {
 371:         replaceStoredTraining(originalTrainingRecords);
 372:       } catch (rollbackError) {
 373:         rollbackErrors.push({
 374:           collection: "training",
 375:           error: rollbackError,
 376:         });
 377:       }
 378: 
 379:       try {
 380:         replaceStoredCertifications(originalCertificationRecords);
 381:       } catch (rollbackError) {
 382:         rollbackErrors.push({
 383:           collection: "certifications",
 384:           error: rollbackError,
 385:         });
 386:       }
 387:     }
 388: 
 389:     throw createRelationshipError(
 390:       rollbackErrors.length > 0
 391:         ? "TRAINING_CERTIFICATION_ROLLBACK_FAILED"
 392:         : "TRAINING_CERTIFICATION_TRANSACTION_FAILED",
 393: 
 394:       "Training and Certification relationship synchronization failed.",
 395: 
 396:       rollbackErrors.length > 0
 397:         ? "The relationship update failed and could not be completely restored. Reload both libraries before making more changes."
 398:         : "The Training and Certification relationship could not be saved.",
 399: 
 400:       {
 401:         rollbackErrors,
 402:         trainingWriteCompleted,
 403:         certificationWriteCompleted,
 404:       },
 405: 
 406:       cause,
 407:     );
 408:   }
 409: }
 410: 
 411: /*
 412:  * =========================================
 413:  * Training-Authoritative Synchronization
 414:  * =========================================
 415:  */
 416: 
 417: export function saveTrainingWithCertificationSync({
 418:   trainingRecords,
 419:   changedTrainingIds = [],
 420:   deletedTrainingIds = [],
 421: } = {}) {
 422:   const originalTrainingRecords = readStoredTraining();
 423: 
 424:   const originalCertificationRecords = readStoredCertifications();
 425: 
 426:   let nextTrainingRecords = normalizeTrainingCollection(trainingRecords);
 427: 
 428:   let nextCertificationRecords = normalizeCertificationCollection(
 429:     originalCertificationRecords,
 430:   );
 431: 
 432:   const changedIds = createIdSet(changedTrainingIds);
 433: 
 434:   const deletedIds = createIdSet(deletedTrainingIds);
 435: 
 436:   const trainingMap = createRecordMap(nextTrainingRecords);
 437: 
 438:   const certificationMap = createRecordMap(nextCertificationRecords);
 439: 
 440:   /*
 441:    * Remove deleted Training records from every
 442:    * Certification relationship collection.
 443:    */
 444: 
 445:   if (deletedIds.size > 0) {
 446:     nextCertificationRecords = nextCertificationRecords.map((certification) => {
 447:       const currentRelationships = getArray(
 448:         certification?.relatedRecords?.trainingRelationships,
 449:       );
 450: 
 451:       const remainingRelationships = currentRelationships.filter(
 452:         (relationship) => !deletedIds.has(getText(relationship?.trainingId)),
 453:       );
 454: 
 455:       if (remainingRelationships.length === currentRelationships.length) {
 456:         return certification;
 457:       }
 458: 
 459:       return normalizeCertification({
 460:         ...certification,
 461: 
 462:         relatedRecords: {
 463:           ...certification.relatedRecords,
 464: 
 465:           trainingRelationships: remainingRelationships,
 466:         },
 467: 
 468:         updatedAt: new Date().toISOString(),
 469:       });
 470:     });
 471:   }
 472: 
 473:   /*
 474:    * For a Training save, Training is authoritative:
 475:    * its selected Certification IDs determine which
 476:    * Certification records should point back to it.
 477:    */
 478: 
 479:   changedIds.forEach((trainingId) => {
 480:     const training = requireTraining(trainingMap, trainingId);
 481: 
 482:     const selectedCertificationIds = new Set(
 483:       getTrainingCertificationIds(training),
 484:     );
 485: 
 486:     selectedCertificationIds.forEach((certificationId) => {
 487:       requireCertification(certificationMap, certificationId);
 488:     });
 489: 
 490:     nextCertificationRecords = nextCertificationRecords.map((certification) =>
 491:       synchronizeCertificationWithTraining(
 492:         certification,
 493:         training,
 494:         selectedCertificationIds.has(certification.id),
 495:       ),
 496:     );
 497:   });
 498: 
 499:   /*
 500:    * Refresh both snapshot directions after the
 501:    * relationship membership is synchronized.
 502:    */
 503: 
 504:   const refreshedCertificationMap = createRecordMap(nextCertificationRecords);
 505: 
 506:   nextTrainingRecords = nextTrainingRecords.map((training) =>
 507:     changedIds.has(training.id)
 508:       ? refreshTrainingCertificationRelationships(
 509:           training,
 510:           refreshedCertificationMap,
 511:         )
 512:       : training,
 513:   );
 514: 
 515:   return commitCollections({
 516:     originalTrainingRecords,
 517:     originalCertificationRecords,
 518:     nextTrainingRecords,
 519:     nextCertificationRecords,
 520:   });
 521: }
 522: 
 523: /*
 524:  * =========================================
 525:  * Certification-Authoritative Synchronization
 526:  * =========================================
 527:  */
 528: 
 529: export function saveCertificationWithTrainingSync({
 530:   certificationRecords,
 531:   changedCertificationIds = [],
 532:   deletedCertificationIds = [],
 533: } = {}) {
 534:   const originalTrainingRecords = readStoredTraining();
 535: 
 536:   const originalCertificationRecords = readStoredCertifications();
 537: 
 538:   let nextTrainingRecords = normalizeTrainingCollection(
 539:     originalTrainingRecords,
 540:   );
 541: 
 542:   let nextCertificationRecords =
 543:     normalizeCertificationCollection(certificationRecords);
 544: 
 545:   const changedIds = createIdSet(changedCertificationIds);
 546: 
 547:   const deletedIds = createIdSet(deletedCertificationIds);
 548: 
 549:   const trainingMap = createRecordMap(nextTrainingRecords);
 550: 
 551:   const certificationMap = createRecordMap(nextCertificationRecords);
 552: 
 553:   /*
 554:    * Remove deleted Certification records from
 555:    * all Training records.
 556:    */
 557: 
 558:   if (deletedIds.size > 0) {
 559:     nextTrainingRecords = nextTrainingRecords.map((training) => {
 560:       const remainingRelationships = getArray(
 561:         training?.certificationRelationships,
 562:       ).filter(
 563:         (relationship) =>
 564:           !deletedIds.has(getText(relationship?.certificationId)),
 565:       );
 566: 
 567:       return {
 568:         ...training,
 569: 
 570:         certificationRelationships: remainingRelationships,
 571: 
 572:         relatedCertificationIds: remainingRelationships.map(
 573:           (relationship) => relationship.certificationId,
 574:         ),
 575:       };
 576:     });
 577:   }
 578: 
 579:   /*
 580:    * For a Certification save, Certification is
 581:    * authoritative: its Training relationships
 582:    * determine which Training records point back.
 583:    */
 584: 
 585:   changedIds.forEach((certificationId) => {
 586:     const certification = requireCertification(
 587:       certificationMap,
 588:       certificationId,
 589:     );
 590: 
 591:     const selectedTrainingIds = new Set(
 592:       getCertificationTrainingIds(certification),
 593:     );
 594: 
 595:     selectedTrainingIds.forEach((trainingId) => {
 596:       requireTraining(trainingMap, trainingId);
 597:     });
 598: 
 599:     nextTrainingRecords = nextTrainingRecords.map((training) =>
 600:       synchronizeTrainingWithCertification(
 601:         training,
 602:         certification,
 603:         selectedTrainingIds.has(training.id),
 604:       ),
 605:     );
 606:   });
 607: 
 608:   const refreshedTrainingMap = createRecordMap(nextTrainingRecords);
 609: 
 610:   nextCertificationRecords = nextCertificationRecords.map((certification) =>
 611:     changedIds.has(certification.id)
 612:       ? refreshCertificationTrainingRelationships(
 613:           certification,
 614:           refreshedTrainingMap,
 615:         )
 616:       : certification,
 617:   );
 618: 
 619:   return commitCollections({
 620:     originalTrainingRecords,
 621:     originalCertificationRecords,
 622:     nextTrainingRecords,
 623:     nextCertificationRecords,
 624:   });
 625: }

FILE: src\services\Training\trainingApiService.js
SHA256: 5DC0063818BEAF6DB02D5F7CCA835BF4C08831EC826B9B113A0D38BAB6CCA1A2
   1: import { apiGet } from "../apiClient.js";
   2: 
   3: const TRAININGS_ENDPOINT = "/trainings";
   4: 
   5: function trainingPath(trainingId) {
   6:   if (
   7:     typeof trainingId !== "string" ||
   8:     !trainingId.trim() ||
   9:     trainingId.trim().length > 200
  10:   ) {
  11:     throw new TypeError("A valid Training ID is required.");
  12:   }
  13: 
  14:   return `${TRAININGS_ENDPOINT}/${encodeURIComponent(
  15:     trainingId.trim(),
  16:   )}`;
  17: }
  18: 
  19: export async function fetchTrainings(options = {}) {
  20:   const response = await apiGet(TRAININGS_ENDPOINT, options);
  21: 
  22:   if (!Array.isArray(response?.data?.trainings)) {
  23:     throw new TypeError("Invalid Training collection API response.");
  24:   }
  25: 
  26:   return response.data.trainings;
  27: }
  28: 
  29: export async function fetchTrainingById(trainingId, options = {}) {
  30:   const response = await apiGet(trainingPath(trainingId), options);
  31: 
  32:   if (
  33:     !response?.data?.training ||
  34:     typeof response.data.training !== "object" ||
  35:     Array.isArray(response.data.training)
  36:   ) {
  37:     throw new TypeError("Invalid Training record API response.");
  38:   }
  39: 
  40:   return response.data.training;
  41: }

  44: function createTrainingCandidate(trainingData = {}) {
  45:   const defaults = createEmptyTraining();
  46: 
  47:   const source =
  48:     trainingData && typeof trainingData === "object" ? trainingData : {};
  49: 
  50:   return {
  51:     ...defaults,
  52:     ...source,
  53: 
  54:     provider: {
  55:       ...defaults.provider,
  56:       ...source.provider,

  58:

  60:       ...defaults.delivery,
  61:       ...source.delivery,

 101: export async function getTrainingById(trainingId) {
 102:   if (!trainingId) {
 103:     throw createTrainingServiceError({
 104:       message: "A training ID is required.",
 105:       publicMessage: "The training record could not be identified.",
 106:       code: "TRAINING_ID_REQUIRED",
 107:       status: 400,
 108:     });
 109:   }
 110: 
 111:   const training = readStoredTraining().find(
 112:     (record) => record.id === trainingId,
 113:   );
 114: 
 115:   if (!training) {
 116:     throw createTrainingNotFoundError(trainingId);
 117:   }
 118: 
 119:   return training;
 120: }

 122: export async function createTraining(trainingData) {
 123:   const records = readStoredTraining();
 124: 
 125:   const candidate = createTrainingCandidate(trainingData);
 126: 
 127:   assertValidTraining(candidate);
 128: 
 129:   const training = createTrainingModel(candidate);
 130: 
 131:   if (records.some((record) => record.id === training.id)) {
 132:     throw createTrainingServiceError({
 133:       message: `Training ID "${training.id}" already exists.`,
 134:       publicMessage:
 135:         "This training record could not be created because its identifier already exists.",
 136:       code: "TRAINING_ID_CONFLICT",
 137:       status: 409,
 138:     });
 139:   }
 140: 
 141:   const transaction = saveTrainingWithCertificationSync({
 142:     trainingRecords: [training, ...records],
 143: 
 144:     changedTrainingIds: [training.id],
 145:   });
 146: 
 147:   return (
 148:     transaction.trainingRecords.find((record) => record.id === training.id) ||
 149:     training
 150:   );
 151: }

 153: export async function updateTraining(trainingId, updates = {}) {
 154:   if (!trainingId) {
 155:     throw createTrainingServiceError({
 156:       message: "A training ID is required.",
 157:       publicMessage: "The training record could not be identified.",
 158:       code: "TRAINING_ID_REQUIRED",
 159:       status: 400,
 160:     });
 161:   }
 162: 
 163:   const records = readStoredTraining();
 164: 
 165:   const recordIndex = records.findIndex(
 166:     (training) => training.id === trainingId,
 167:   );
 168: 
 169:   if (recordIndex === -1) {
 170:     throw createTrainingNotFoundError(trainingId);
 171:   }
 172: 
 173:   const currentTraining = records[recordIndex];
 174: 
 175:   const candidate = createTrainingCandidate({
 176:     ...currentTraining,
 177:     ...updates,
 178: 
 179:     provider: {
 180:       ...currentTraining.provider,
 181:       ...updates.provider,
 182:     },
 183: 
 184:     delivery: {
 185:       ...currentTraining.delivery,
 186:       ...updates.delivery,
 187: 
 188:       location: {
 189:         ...currentTraining.delivery?.location,
 190:         ...updates.delivery?.location,
 191:       },
 192:     },
 193: 
 194:     dates: {
 195:       ...currentTraining.dates,
 196:       ...updates.dates,
 197:     },
 198: 
 199:     completion: {
 200:       ...currentTraining.completion,
 201:       ...updates.completion,
 202:     },
 203: 
 204:     visibility: {
 205:       ...currentTraining.visibility,
 206:       ...updates.visibility,
 207:     },
 208: 
 209:     privateInformation: {
 210:       ...currentTraining.privateInformation,
 211:       ...updates.privateInformation,
 212:     },
 213:   });
 214: 
 215:   assertValidTraining(candidate);
 216: 
 217:   const updatedTraining = updateTrainingModel(currentTraining, updates);
 218: 
 219:   const nextRecords = [...records];
 220: 
 221:   nextRecords[recordIndex] = updatedTraining;
 222: 
 223:   const transaction = saveTrainingWithCertificationSync({
 224:     trainingRecords: nextRecords,
 225: 
 226:     changedTrainingIds: [updatedTraining.id],
 227:   });
 228: 
 229:   return (
 230:     transaction.trainingRecords.find(
 231:       (record) => record.id === updatedTraining.id,
 232:     ) || updatedTraining
 233:   );
 234: }
 235: 
 236: export async function archiveTraining(trainingId) {
 237:   return updateTraining(trainingId, {

 239:   });
 240: }

 242: export async function restoreTraining(trainingId) {
 243:   return updateTraining(trainingId, {
 244:     status: "active",
 245:   });

 248: function getTrainingDocumentStorageKeys(training) {
 249:   const documents = Array.isArray(training?.supportingDocuments)
 250:     ? training.supportingDocuments
 251:     : [];
 252: 
 253:   return [
 254:     ...new Set(
 255:       documents
 256:         .map((document) =>
 257:           typeof document?.storageKey === "string"
 258:             ? document.storageKey.trim()
 259:             : "",
 260:         )
 261:         .filter(Boolean),
 262:     ),
 263:   ];
 264: }
