 266: export async function deleteTraining(trainingId) {
 267:   if (!trainingId) {
 268:     throw createTrainingServiceError({
 269:       message: "A training ID is required.",
 270:       publicMessage: "The training record could not be identified.",
 271:       code: "TRAINING_ID_REQUIRED",
 272:       status: 400,
 273:     });
 274:   }
 275: 
 276:   const records = readStoredTraining();
 277: 
 278:   const recordToDelete = records.find((training) => training.id === trainingId);
 279: 
 280:   if (!recordToDelete) {
 281:     throw createTrainingNotFoundError(trainingId);
 282:   }
 283: 
 284:   const storageKeys = getTrainingDocumentStorageKeys(recordToDelete);
 285: 
 286:   const remainingRecords = records.filter(
 287:     (training) => training.id !== trainingId,
 288:   );
 289: 
 290:   /*
 291:    * Remove Training metadata and reciprocal
 292:    * Certification links first.
 293:    */
 294: 
 295:   saveTrainingWithCertificationSync({
 296:     trainingRecords: remainingRecords,
 297: 
 298:     deletedTrainingIds: [trainingId],
 299:   });
 300: 
 301:   let documentCleanup = null;
 302: 
 303:   try {
 304:     documentCleanup = await deleteStoredDocuments(storageKeys);
 305:   } catch (cleanupError) {
 306:     /*
 307:      * Metadata and relationships are already
 308:      * consistently deleted. Failed binary cleanup
 309:      * can be handled later as orphan maintenance.
 310:      */
 311: 
 312:     documentCleanup = {
 313:       completed: false,
 314:       storageKeys,
 315:       error:
 316:         cleanupError?.publicMessage ||
 317:         cleanupError?.message ||
 318:         "Document cleanup failed.",
 319:     };
 320:   }
 321: 
 322:   return {
 323:     id: trainingId,
 324:     title: recordToDelete.title,
 325:     providerName: recordToDelete.provider?.name || "",
 326:     deleted: true,
 327:     documents: documentCleanup,
 328:   };
 329: }

 331: export async function validateTrainingDraft(
 332:   trainingData,
 333:   validationOptions = {},
 334: ) {
 335:   return validateTraining(
 336:     createTrainingCandidate(trainingData),
 337:     validationOptions,
 338:   );
 339: }
 340: 
 341: export async function replaceTrainingRecords(trainingValues) {
 342:   if (!Array.isArray(trainingValues)) {
 343:     throw createTrainingServiceError({
 344:       message: "The replacement Training Library must be an array.",
 345: 
 346:       publicMessage: "The Training Library has an invalid format.",
 347: 
 348:       code: "INVALID_TRAINING_COLLECTION",
 349: 
 350:       status: 400,
 351:     });
 352:   }
 353: 
 354:   const currentRecords = readStoredTraining();
 355: 
 356:   const validatedRecords = trainingValues.map((value) => {
 357:     const candidate = createTrainingCandidate(value);
 358: 
 359:     assertValidTraining(candidate);
 360: 
 361:     return normalizeTraining(candidate);
 362:   });
 363: 
 364:   const normalizedRecords = normalizeTrainingCollection(validatedRecords);
 365: 
 366:   const nextTrainingIds = new Set(
 367:     normalizedRecords.map((training) => training.id),
 368:   );
 369: 
 370:   const deletedTrainingIds = currentRecords
 371:     .map((training) => training.id)
 372:     .filter((trainingId) => !nextTrainingIds.has(trainingId));
 373: 
 374:   const transaction = saveTrainingWithCertificationSync({
 375:     trainingRecords: normalizedRecords,
 376: 
 377:     changedTrainingIds: normalizedRecords.map((training) => training.id),
 378: 
 379:     deletedTrainingIds,
 380:   });
 381: 
 382:   return transaction.trainingRecords;
 383: }

SHA256: AA66BD298CC8D749DE5BDDA354961041C5864F8A81717FD23B46BC0CBD9B236F

   2:   normalizeTrainingCollection,
   3:   TRAINING_MODEL_VERSION,
   4: } from "../../models/trainingModel.js";
   5: 
   6: export const TRAINING_STORAGE_KEY = "portfolio-platform:training";
   7: 
   8: const TRAINING_STORAGE_VERSION = 1;
   9: 
  10: function createTrainingStorageError(message, publicMessage, cause = null) {
  11:   const error = new Error(message);
  12: 
  13:   error.name = "TrainingStorageError";
  14:   error.code = "TRAINING_STORAGE_ERROR";
  15:   error.publicMessage = publicMessage;
  16: 
  17:   if (cause) {
  18:     error.cause = cause;
  19:   }
  20: 
  21:   return error;
  22: }
  23: 
  24: function getBrowserStorage() {
  25:   if (typeof window === "undefined" || !window.localStorage) {
  26:     throw createTrainingStorageError(
  27:       "Training storage is unavailable.",
  28:       "Training storage is not supported in this environment.",
  29:     );
  30:   }
  31: 
  32:   return window.localStorage;
  33: }
  34: 
  35: function createEmptyTrainingStorageDocument() {
  36:   return {
  37:     storageVersion: TRAINING_STORAGE_VERSION,
  38:     trainingModelVersion: TRAINING_MODEL_VERSION,
  39:     trainingRecords: [],
  40:     updatedAt: new Date().toISOString(),
  41:   };
  42: }
  43: 
  44: function parseTrainingStorageDocument(value) {
  45:   if (!value) {
  46:     return createEmptyTrainingStorageDocument();
  47:   }
  48: 
  49:   let parsedValue;
  50: 
  51:   try {
  52:     parsedValue = JSON.parse(value);
  53:   } catch (cause) {
  54:     throw createTrainingStorageError(
  55:       "Unable to parse stored training data.",
  56:       "The saved Training Library could not be read.",
  57:       cause,
  58:     );
  59:   }
  60: 
  61:   if (Array.isArray(parsedValue)) {
  62:     return {
  63:       ...createEmptyTrainingStorageDocument(),
  64:       trainingRecords: normalizeTrainingCollection(parsedValue),
  65:     };
  66:   }
  67: 
  68:   if (!parsedValue || typeof parsedValue !== "object") {
  69:     throw createTrainingStorageError(
  70:       "Stored training data has an invalid structure.",
  71:       "The saved Training Library has an invalid format.",
  72:     );
  73:   }
  74: 
  75:   return {
  76:     storageVersion:
  77:       Number(parsedValue.storageVersion) || TRAINING_STORAGE_VERSION,
  78: 
  79:     trainingModelVersion:
  80:       Number(parsedValue.trainingModelVersion) || TRAINING_MODEL_VERSION,
  81: 
  82:     trainingRecords: normalizeTrainingCollection(
  83:       parsedValue.trainingRecords ||
  84:         parsedValue.training ||
  85:         parsedValue.records,
  86:     ),
  87: 
  88:     updatedAt:
  89:       typeof parsedValue.updatedAt === "string"
  90:         ? parsedValue.updatedAt
  91:         : new Date().toISOString(),
  92:   };
  93: }
  94: 
  95: export function readTrainingStorage() {
  96:   const storage = getBrowserStorage();
  97: 
  98:   try {
  99:     return parseTrainingStorageDocument(storage.getItem(TRAINING_STORAGE_KEY));
 100:   } catch (error) {
 101:     if (error?.name === "TrainingStorageError") {
 102:       throw error;
 103:     }
 104: 
 105:     throw createTrainingStorageError(
 106:       "Unable to read Training Library storage.",
 107:       "Your saved training records could not be loaded.",
 108:       error,
 109:     );
 110:   }
 111: }
 112: 
 113: export function writeTrainingStorage(trainingRecords) {
 114:   const storage = getBrowserStorage();
 115: 
 116:   const document = {
 117:     storageVersion: TRAINING_STORAGE_VERSION,
 118:     trainingModelVersion: TRAINING_MODEL_VERSION,
 119:     trainingRecords: normalizeTrainingCollection(trainingRecords),
 120:     updatedAt: new Date().toISOString(),
 121:   };
 122: 
 123:   try {
 124:     storage.setItem(TRAINING_STORAGE_KEY, JSON.stringify(document));
 125: 
 126:     return document;
 127:   } catch (error) {
 128:     throw createTrainingStorageError(
 129:       "Unable to write Training Library storage.",
 130:       "Your training records could not be saved.",
 131:       error,
 132:     );
 133:   }
 134: }
 135: 
 136: export function readStoredTraining() {
 137:   return readTrainingStorage().trainingRecords;
 138: }
 139: 
 140: export function replaceStoredTraining(trainingRecords) {
 141:   return writeTrainingStorage(trainingRecords).trainingRecords;
 142: }
 143: 
 144: export function updateStoredTraining(updater) {
 145:   if (typeof updater !== "function") {
 146:     throw createTrainingStorageError(
 147:       "The training updater must be a function.",
 148:       "The Training Library could not be updated.",
 149:     );
 150:   }
 151: 
 152:   const nextRecords = updater([...readStoredTraining()]);
 153: 
 154:   if (!Array.isArray(nextRecords)) {
 155:     throw createTrainingStorageError(
 156:       "The training updater did not return an array.",
 157:       "The Training Library could not be updated.",
 158:     );
 159:   }
 160: 
 161:   return replaceStoredTraining(nextRecords);
 162: }
 163: 
 164: export function clearTrainingStorage() {
 165:   try {
 166:     getBrowserStorage().removeItem(TRAINING_STORAGE_KEY);
 167:   } catch (error) {
 168:     throw createTrainingStorageError(
 169:       "Unable to clear Training Library storage.",
 170:       "The saved Training Library could not be cleared.",
 171:       error,
 172:     );
 173:   }
 174: }
 175: 
 176: export function getTrainingStorageInformation() {
 177:   const document = readTrainingStorage();
 178: 
 179:   return {
 180:     storageKey: TRAINING_STORAGE_KEY,
 181:     storageVersion: document.storageVersion,
 182:     trainingModelVersion: document.trainingModelVersion,
 183:     trainingCount: document.trainingRecords.length,
 184:     updatedAt: document.updatedAt,
 185:   };
 186: }

  66: export function formatTrainingDate(
  67:   value,
  68:   { locale = "en-US", fallback = "" } = {},
  69: ) {
  70:   const text = getText(value);
  71: 
  72:   const match = text.match(
  73:     /^(\d{4})-(0[1-9]|1[0-2])(?:-(0[1-9]|[12]\d|3[01]))?$/,
  74:   );
  75: 
  76:   if (!match) {
  77:     return fallback;
  78:   }
  79: 
  80:   const date = new Date(
  81:     Date.UTC(
  82:       Number(match[1]),
  83:       Number(match[2]) - 1,
  84:       match[3] ? Number(match[3]) : 1,
  85:     ),
  86:   );
  87: 
  88:   return new Intl.DateTimeFormat(locale, {
  89:     month: "short",
  90:     year: "numeric",
  91:     timeZone: "UTC",
  92:   }).format(date);
  93: }
  94: 
  95: export function formatTrainingDateRange(training) {
  96:   const startDate = formatTrainingDate(training?.dates?.startDate);
  97: 
  98:   const endDate = training?.dates?.isCurrent
  99:     ? "Present"
 100:     : formatTrainingDate(training?.dates?.endDate);
 101: 
 102:   if (startDate && endDate) {
 103:     return `${startDate} â€“ ${endDate}`;
 104:   }
 105: 
 106:   return startDate || endDate || "Dates not provided";
 107: }

 109: export function formatTrainingLocation(training) {
 110:   const location = training?.delivery?.location || {};
 111: 
 112:   if (getText(location.displayValue)) {
 113:     return getText(location.displayValue);
 114:   }
 115: 
 116:   return [
 117:     getText(location.city),
 118:     getText(location.stateRegion),
 119:     getText(location.country),
 120:   ]
 121:     .filter(Boolean)
 122:     .join(", ");
 123: }

 125: export function formatTrainingDuration(training) {
 126:   const duration = Number(training?.completion?.durationHours);
 127: 
 128:   if (!Number.isFinite(duration) || duration < 0) {
 129:     return "";
 130:   }
 131: 
 132:   return `${duration} ${duration === 1 ? "hour" : "hours"}`;
 133: }

 135: export function createTrainingSearchText(training) {
 136:   return [
 137:     training?.title,
 138:     training?.trainingType,
 139:     training?.provider?.name,
 140:     training?.provider?.type,
 141:     training?.description,
 142:     training?.completion?.status,
 143:     training?.completion?.credentialId,
 144:     formatTrainingLocation(training),
 145:     getTrainingTypeDisplay(training),
 146:     getTrainingProviderTypeDisplay(training),
 147:     getTrainingDeliveryFormatDisplay(training),
 148:     ...getArray(training?.instructors).flatMap((item) => [
 149:       item?.name,
 150:       item?.title,
 151:       item?.organization,
 152:     ]),
 153:     ...getArray(training?.topics).flatMap((item) => [
 154:       item?.name,
 155:       item?.description,
 156:     ]),
 157:     ...getArray(training?.learningOutcomes).map((item) => item?.text),
 158:     ...getArray(training?.skillRelationships).map(
 159:       (item) => item?.nameSnapshot,
 160:     ),
 161:   ]
 162:     .map(getText)
 163:     .filter(Boolean)
 164:     .join(" ")
 165:     .normalize("NFKC")
 166:     .toLocaleLowerCase();
 167: }
 168: 
 169: export function searchTraining(records = [], query = "") {
 170:   const terms = getText(query)
 171:     .normalize("NFKC")
 172:     .toLocaleLowerCase()
 173:     .split(/\s+/)
 174:     .filter(Boolean);
 175: 
 176:   if (terms.length === 0) {
 177:     return [...getArray(records)];
 178:   }
 179: 
 180:   return getArray(records).filter((training) => {
 181:     const searchText = createTrainingSearchText(training);
 182: 
 183:     return terms.every((term) => searchText.includes(term));
 184:   });
 185: }

 187: export function filterTraining(records = [], filters = {}) {
 188:   const {
 189:     trainingType = "all",
 190:     providerType = "all",
 191:     deliveryFormat = "all",
 192:     completionStatus = "all",
 193:     hasCredential = false,
 194:     hasSkills = false,
 195:   } = filters;
 196: 
 197:   return getArray(records).filter((training) => {
 198:     if (
 199:       trainingType !== "all" &&
 200:       training?.trainingType !== trainingType
 201:     ) {
 202:       return false;
 203:     }
 204: 
 205:     if (
 206:       providerType !== "all" &&
 207:       training?.provider?.type !== providerType
 208:     ) {
 209:       return false;
 210:     }
 211: 
 212:     if (
 213:       deliveryFormat !== "all" &&
 214:       training?.delivery?.format !== deliveryFormat
 215:     ) {
 216:       return false;
 217:     }
 218: 
 219:     if (
 220:       completionStatus !== "all" &&
 221:       training?.completion?.status !== completionStatus
 222:     ) {
 223:       return false;
 224:     }
 225: 
 226:     if (
 227:       hasCredential &&
 228:       !training?.completion?.certificateEarned &&
 229:       !getText(training?.completion?.credentialId) &&
 230:       !getText(training?.completion?.credentialUrl)
 231:     ) {
 232:       return false;
 233:     }
 234: 
 235:     if (
 236:       hasSkills &&
 237:       getArray(training?.skillRelationships).length === 0
 238:     ) {
 239:       return false;
 240:     }
 241: 
 242:     return true;
 243:   });
 244: }
 245: 
 246: function getDateTime(value) {
 247:   const time = new Date(value || 0).getTime();
 248: 
 249:   return Number.isFinite(time) ? time : 0;
 250: }
 251: 
 252: export function sortTraining(records = [], sortOption = "newest") {
 253:   const sorted = [...getArray(records)];
 254: 
 255:   sorted.sort((first, second) => {
 256:     switch (sortOption) {
 257:       case "oldest":
 258:         return (
 259:           getDateTime(first?.dates?.startDate) -
 260:           getDateTime(second?.dates?.startDate)
 261:         );
 262: 
 263:       case "title-ascending":
 264:         return getTrainingTitle(first).localeCompare(
 265:           getTrainingTitle(second),
 266:           undefined,
 267:           { sensitivity: "base" },
 268:         );
 269: 
 270:       case "provider-ascending":
 271:         return getTrainingProviderName(first).localeCompare(
 272:           getTrainingProviderName(second),
 273:           undefined,
 274:           { sensitivity: "base" },
 275:         );
 276: 
 277:       case "recently-updated":
 278:         return (
 279:           getDateTime(second?.updatedAt) -
 280:           getDateTime(first?.updatedAt)
 281:         );
 282: 
 283:       case "newest":
 284:       default:
 285:         if (
 286:           first?.dates?.isCurrent !== second?.dates?.isCurrent
 287:         ) {
 288:           return first?.dates?.isCurrent ? -1 : 1;
 289:         }
 290: 
 291:         return (
 292:           getDateTime(
 293:             second?.dates?.endDate || second?.dates?.startDate,
 294:           ) -
 295:           getDateTime(
 296:             first?.dates?.endDate || first?.dates?.startDate,
 297:           )
 298:         );
 299:     }
 300:   });
 301: 
 302:   return sorted;
 303: }
 304: 
 305: export function prepareTrainingCollection({
 306:   trainingRecords = [],
 307:   query = "",
 308:   filters = {},
 309:   sortOption = "newest",
 310: } = {}) {
 311:   return sortTraining(
 312:     filterTraining(searchTraining(trainingRecords, query), filters),
 313:     sortOption,
 314:   );
 315: }
 316: 
 317: export function getTrainingCompleteness(training) {
 318:   const checks = [
 319:     {
 320:       id: "title",
 321:       label: "Training title",
 322:       complete: Boolean(getText(training?.title)),
 323:       weight: 2,
 324:     },
 325:     {
 326:       id: "provider",
 327:       label: "Provider",
 328:       complete: Boolean(getText(training?.provider?.name)),
 329:       weight: 2,
 330:     },
 331:     {
 332:       id: "dates",
 333:       label: "Training dates",
 334:       complete: Boolean(
 335:         getText(training?.dates?.startDate) &&
 336:           (training?.dates?.isCurrent ||
 337:             getText(training?.dates?.endDate)),
 338:       ),
 339:       weight: 2,
 340:     },
 341:     {
 342:       id: "description",
 343:       label: "Description",
 344:       complete: Boolean(getText(training?.description)),
 345:       weight: 1,
 346:     },
 347:     {
 348:       id: "topics",
 349:       label: "Topics",
 350:       complete: getArray(training?.topics).length > 0,
 351:       weight: 1,
 352:     },
 353:     {
 354:       id: "outcomes",
 355:       label: "Learning outcomes",
 356:       complete: getArray(training?.learningOutcomes).length > 0,
 357:       weight: 1,
 358:     },
 359:     {
 360:       id: "skills",
 361:       label: "Related skills",
 362:       complete: getArray(training?.skillRelationships).length > 0,
 363:       weight: 1,
 364:     },
 365:     {
 366:       id: "evidence",
 367:       label: "Credential or supporting document",
 368:       complete: Boolean(
 369:         training?.completion?.certificateEarned ||
 370:           getText(training?.completion?.credentialId) ||
 371:           getText(training?.completion?.credentialUrl) ||
 372:           getArray(training?.supportingDocuments).length > 0,
 373:       ),
 374:       weight: 1,
 375:     },
 376:   ];
 377: 
 378:   const maximumScore = checks.reduce(
 379:     (total, check) => total + check.weight,
 380:     0,
 381:   );
 382: 
 383:   const score = checks.reduce(
 384:     (total, check) => total + (check.complete ? check.weight : 0),
 385:     0,
 386:   );
 387: 
 388:   const percentage = Math.round((score / maximumScore) * 100);
 389: 
 390:   let level = "basic";
 391:   let label = "Basic";
 392: 
 393:   if (percentage >= 90) {
 394:     level = "excellent";
 395:     label = "Excellent";
 396:   } else if (percentage >= 70) {
 397:     level = "strong";
 398:     label = "Strong";
 399:   } else if (percentage >= 50) {
 400:     level = "developing";
 401:     label = "Developing";
 402:   }
 403: 
 404:   return {
 405:     score,
 406:     maximumScore,
 407:     percentage,
 408:     level,
 409:     label,
 410:     checks,
 411:     completedChecks: checks.filter((check) => check.complete),
 412:     missingChecks: checks.filter((check) => !check.complete),
 413:   };
 414: }
 415: 
 416: export function getTrainingStatistics(trainingRecords = []) {
 417:   const collection = getArray(trainingRecords);
 418: 
 419:   const active = getActiveTraining(collection);
 420: 
 421:   const archived = getArchivedTraining(collection);
 422: 
 423:   return {
 424:     total: collection.length,
 425:     active: active.length,
 426:     archived: archived.length,
 427: 
 428:     planned: active.filter(
 429:       (training) => training.completion?.status === "planned",
 430:     ).length,
 431: 
 432:     inProgress: active.filter(
 433:       (training) => training.completion?.status === "in-progress",
 434:     ).length,
 435: 
 436:     completed: active.filter(
 437:       (training) => training.completion?.status === "completed",
 438:     ).length,
 439: 
 440:     withCredentials: active.filter(
 441:       (training) =>
 442:         training.completion?.certificateEarned === true ||
 443:         Boolean(getText(training.completion?.credentialId)) ||
 444:         Boolean(getText(training.completion?.credentialUrl)),
 445:     ).length,
 446: 
 447:     withSkills: active.filter(
 448:       (training) =>
 449:         getArray(training.skillRelationships).length > 0,
 450:     ).length,
 451: 
 452:     withDocuments: active.filter(
 453:       (training) =>
 454:         getArray(training.supportingDocuments).length > 0,
 455:     ).length,
 456:   };
 457: }

  53: function validateTextLength(collector, field, value, limit, label) {
  54:   if (typeof value === "string" && value.trim().length > limit) {
  55:     collector.addError(
  56:       field,
  57:       `${label} cannot exceed ${limit} characters.`,
  58:       "too_long",
  59:     );
  60:   }

  63: function validateOrder(collector, field, value) {
  64:   if (
  65:     value !== undefined &&
  66:     value !== null &&
  67:     (!Number.isInteger(value) || value < 0)
  68:   ) {
  69:     collector.addError(
  70:       field,
  71:       "Item order must be a whole number of zero or greater.",
  72:       "invalid_order",
  73:     );
  74:   }
  75: }
  76: 
  77: const DATE_PATTERN = /^\d{4}-(0[1-9]|1[0-2])(?:-(0[1-9]|[12]\d|3[01]))?$/;
  78: 
  79: const CERTIFICATION_SNAPSHOT_LIMITS = Object.freeze({
  80:   name: 160,
  81:   issuingOrganizationName: 160,
  82:   certificationType: 100,
  83:   credentialState: 100,
  84: });

  86: const VALID_CERTIFICATION_STATES = new Set(["active", "expired", "planned"]);

  88: function isValidDate(value) {
  89:   const text = getText(value);
  90: 
  91:   if (!DATE_PATTERN.test(text)) {
  92:     return false;
  93:   }
  94: 
  95:   if (text.length === 7) {
  96:     return true;
  97:   }
  98: 
  99:   const [year, month, day] = text.split("-").map(Number);
 100:   const date = new Date(Date.UTC(year, month - 1, day));
 101: 
 102:   return (
 103:     date.getUTCFullYear() === year &&
 104:     date.getUTCMonth() === month - 1 &&
 105:     date.getUTCDate() === day
 106:   );
 107: }
 108: 
 109: function getComparableDate(value, endOfMonth = false) {
 110:   if (!isValidDate(value)) {
 111:     return null;
 112:   }
 113: 
 114:   const [year, month, suppliedDay] = value.split("-").map(Number);
 115: 
 116:   const day =
 117:     suppliedDay ||
 118:     (endOfMonth ? new Date(Date.UTC(year, month, 0)).getUTCDate() : 1);
 119: 
 120:   return Date.UTC(year, month - 1, day);
 121: }
 122: 
 123: function isValidUrl(value) {
 124:   const text = getText(value);
 125: 
 126:   if (!text) {
 127:     return true;
 128:   }
 129: 
 130:   try {
 131:     const url = new URL(/^https?:\/\//i.test(text) ? text : `https://${text}`);
 132: 
 133:     return ["http:", "https:"].includes(url.protocol) && Boolean(url.hostname);
 134:   } catch {
 135:     return false;
 136:   }
 137: }

 319: function validateDates(collector, training) {
 320:   const startDate = getText(training.dates?.startDate);
 321:   const endDate = getText(training.dates?.endDate);
 322:   const isCurrent = training.dates?.isCurrent === true;
 323: 
 324:   if (startDate && !isValidDate(startDate)) {
 325:     collector.addError(
 326:       "dates.startDate",
 327:       "Enter a valid start date.",
 328:       "invalid_date",
 329:     );
 330:   }
 331: 
 332:   if (isCurrent && endDate) {
 333:     collector.addError(
 334:       "dates.endDate",
 335:       "Current training cannot have an end date.",
 336:       "current_training_has_end_date",
 337:     );
 338:   }
 339: 
 340:   if (!isCurrent && !endDate) {
 341:     collector.addError(
 342:       "dates.endDate",
 343:       "Enter an end date or mark the training as current.",
 344:       "required",
 345:     );
 346:   }
 347: 
 348:   if (endDate && !isValidDate(endDate)) {
 349:     collector.addError(
 350:       "dates.endDate",
 351:       "Enter a valid end date.",
 352:       "invalid_date",
 353:     );
 354:   }
 355: 
 356:   if (
 357:     isValidDate(startDate) &&
 358:     isValidDate(endDate) &&
 359:     getComparableDate(endDate, true) < getComparableDate(startDate)
 360:   ) {
 361:     collector.addError(
 362:       "dates.endDate",
 363:       "The end date cannot be earlier than the start date.",
 364:       "invalid_date_order",
 365:     );
 366:   }
 367: }

 369: function validateCompletion(collector, training) {
 370:   const completion = getObject(training.completion);
 371:   const duration = completion.durationHours;
 372: 
 373:   if (duration !== null && duration !== undefined && duration !== "") {
 374:     const numericDuration = Number(duration);
 375: 
 376:     if (!Number.isFinite(numericDuration) || numericDuration < 0) {
 377:       collector.addError(
 378:         "completion.durationHours",
 379:         "Duration must be zero or greater.",
 380:         "invalid_number",
 381:       );
 382:     } else if (numericDuration > TRAINING_FIELD_LIMITS.maximumDurationHours) {
 383:       collector.addError(
 384:         "completion.durationHours",
 385:         `Duration cannot exceed ${TRAINING_FIELD_LIMITS.maximumDurationHours} hours.`,
 386:         "invalid_number",
 387:       );
 388:     }
 389:   }
 390: 
 391:   validateTextLength(
 392:     collector,
 393:     "completion.credentialId",
 394:     completion.credentialId,
 395:     TRAINING_FIELD_LIMITS.credentialId,
 396:     "Credential ID",
 397:   );
 398: 
 399:   validateTextLength(
 400:     collector,
 401:     "completion.credentialUrl",
 402:     completion.credentialUrl,
 403:     TRAINING_FIELD_LIMITS.credentialUrl,
 404:     "Credential URL",
 405:   );
 406: 
 407:   if (!isValidUrl(completion.credentialUrl)) {
 408:     collector.addError(
 409:       "completion.credentialUrl",
 410:       "Enter a valid credential URL.",
 411:       "invalid_url",
 412:     );
 413:   }
 414: 
 415:   if (
 416:     completion.certificateEarned === true &&
 417:     getCertificationRelationships(training).length === 0 &&
 418:     !getText(completion.credentialId) &&
 419:     !getText(completion.credentialUrl) &&
 420:     getArray(training.supportingDocuments).length === 0
 421:   ) {
 422:     collector.addWarning(
 423:       "completion.certificateEarned",
 424:       "Consider adding a credential ID, verification URL, or certificate document.",
 425:       "missing_credential_evidence",
 426:     );
 427:   }
 428: }
 429: 
 430: function validateLocation(collector, training) {
 431:   const location = getObject(training.delivery?.location);
 432: 
 433:   [
 434:     ["city", TRAINING_FIELD_LIMITS.city, "City"],
 435:     ["stateRegion", TRAINING_FIELD_LIMITS.stateRegion, "State or region"],
 436:     ["country", TRAINING_FIELD_LIMITS.country, "Country"],
 437:     [
 438:       "displayValue",
 439:       TRAINING_FIELD_LIMITS.locationDisplayValue,
 440:       "Display location",
 441:     ],
 442:   ].forEach(([field, limit, label]) => {
 443:     validateTextLength(
 444:       collector,
 445:       `delivery.location.${field}`,
 446:       location[field],
 447:       limit,
 448:       label,
 449:     );
 450:   });
 451: }

 453: function validateOrderedItems(
 454:   collector,
 455:   values,
 456:   { field, maximumItems, textField, textLimit, label },
 457: ) {
 458:   const items = getArray(values);
 459: 
 460:   if (items.length > maximumItems) {
 461:     collector.addError(
 462:       field,
 463:       `Add no more than ${maximumItems} ${label.toLowerCase()} records.`,
 464:       "too_many_items",
 465:     );
 466:   }
 467: 
 468:   const identities = new Set();
 469: 
 470:   items.forEach((item, index) => {
 471:     const object = getObject(item);
 472: 
 473:     const text = getText(typeof item === "string" ? item : object[textField]);
 474: 
 475:     if (!text) {
 476:       collector.addError(
 477:         `${field}.${index}.${textField}`,
 478:         `${label} cannot be empty.`,
 479:         "required",
 480:       );
 481:     }
 482: 
 483:     validateTextLength(
 484:       collector,
 485:       `${field}.${index}.${textField}`,
 486:       text,
 487:       textLimit,
 488:       label,
 489:     );
 490: 
 491:     validateOrder(collector, `${field}.${index}.order`, object.order);
 492: 
 493:     const identity = text.toLocaleLowerCase();
 494: 
 495:     if (identity && identities.has(identity)) {
 496:       collector.addError(
 497:         `${field}.${index}.${textField}`,
 498:         `This ${label.toLowerCase()} has already been added.`,
 499:         "duplicate",
 500:       );
 501:     }
 502: 
 503:     identities.add(identity);
 504:   });
 505: }

 512:     textLimit: TRAINING_FIELD_LIMITS.instructorName,
 513:     label: "Instructor",
 514:   });

 519:     textField: "name",
 520:     textLimit: TRAINING_FIELD_LIMITS.topicName,
 521:     label: "Topic",
 522:   });
 523: 
 524:   validateOrderedItems(collector, training.learningOutcomes, {
 525:     field: "learningOutcomes",
 526:     maximumItems: TRAINING_FIELD_LIMITS.maximumLearningOutcomes,
 527:     textField: "text",
 528:     textLimit: TRAINING_FIELD_LIMITS.outcomeText,

 533: function validateSkillRelationships(collector, training) {
 534:   const relationships = getArray(training.skillRelationships);
 535:   const identities = new Set();
 536: 
 537:   if (relationships.length > TRAINING_FIELD_LIMITS.maximumSkills) {
 538:     collector.addError(
 539:       "skillRelationships",
 540:       `Add no more than ${TRAINING_FIELD_LIMITS.maximumSkills} skills.`,
 541:       "too_many_items",
 542:     );
 543:   }
 544: 
 545:   relationships.forEach((relationship, index) => {
 546:     const item = getObject(relationship);
 547:     const skillId = getText(item.skillId || item.profileSkillId);
 548:     const name = getText(item.nameSnapshot || item.name);
 549: 
 550:     if (!skillId && !name) {
 551:       collector.addError(
 552:         `skillRelationships.${index}.skillId`,
 553:         "Select a saved skill or provide a skill name.",
 554:         "required",
 555:       );
 556:     }
 557: 
 558:     if (skillId && !name) {
 559:       collector.addError(
 560:         `skillRelationships.${index}.nameSnapshot`,
 561:         "Store the skill name snapshot with this relationship.",
 562:         "required_snapshot",
 563:       );
 564:     }
 565: 
 566:     if (!skillId && name) {
 567:       collector.addWarning(
 568:         `skillRelationships.${index}.skillId`,
 569:         `"${name}" is not connected to the central Skill Library.`,
 570:         "missing_skill_relationship",
 571:       );
 572:     }
 573: 
 574:     const identity = (
 575:       skillId ? `id:${skillId}` : `name:${name}`
 576:     ).toLocaleLowerCase();
 577: 
 578:     if (identities.has(identity)) {
 579:       collector.addError(
 580:         `skillRelationships.${index}.skillId`,
 581:         "This skill has already been added.",
 582:         "duplicate",
 583:       );
 584:     }
 585: 
 586:     identities.add(identity);
 587: 
 588:     validateOrder(collector, `skillRelationships.${index}.order`, item.order);
 589:   });
 590: }
 591: 
 592: /*
 593:  * =========================================
 594:  * Certification Relationships
 595:  * =========================================
 596:  */
 597: 
 598: function validateCertificationRelationships(
 599:   collector,
 600:   training,
 601:   certifications,
 602:   shouldCheckCertifications,
 603: ) {
 604:   const relationships = getCertificationRelationships(training);
 605: 
 606:   const availableCertificationIds = getCertificationIdSet(certifications);
 607: 
 608:   const usedCertificationIds = new Set();
 609: 
 610:   if (
 611:     relationships.length > TRAINING_FIELD_LIMITS.maximumRelatedCertifications
 612:   ) {
 613:     collector.addError(
 614:       "certificationRelationships",
 615:       `Link no more than ${TRAINING_FIELD_LIMITS.maximumRelatedCertifications} Certifications.`,
 616:       "too_many_certification_relationships",
 617:     );
 618:   }
 619: 
 620:   relationships.forEach((relationship, index) => {
 621:     const item = getObject(relationship);
 622: 
 623:     const certificationId = getText(
 624:       item.certificationId || item.certificationRecordId || item.credentialId,
 625:     );
 626: 
 627:     const snapshot = getObject(item.snapshot);
 628: 
 629:     const certificationIdField = `certificationRelationships.${index}.certificationId`;
 630: 
 631:     const snapshotField = `certificationRelationships.${index}.snapshot`;
 632: 
 633:     if (!certificationId) {
 634:       collector.addError(
 635:         certificationIdField,
 636:         "Select a valid Certification Library record.",
 637:         "certification_id_required",
 638:       );
 639: 
 640:       return;
 641:     }
 642: 
 643:     const comparisonId = certificationId.toLocaleLowerCase();
 644: 
 645:     if (usedCertificationIds.has(comparisonId)) {
 646:       collector.addError(
 647:         certificationIdField,
 648:         "This Certification has already been linked.",
 649:         "duplicate_certification_relationship",
 650:       );
 651:     } else {
 652:       usedCertificationIds.add(comparisonId);
 653:     }
 654: 
 655:     validateOrder(
 656:       collector,
 657:       `certificationRelationships.${index}.order`,
 658:       item.order,
 659:     );
 660: 
 661:     /*
 662:      * Readable snapshot validation
 663:      */
 664: 
 665:     const snapshotName = getText(
 666:       snapshot.name ||
 667:         snapshot.title ||
 668:         item.nameSnapshot ||
 669:         item.certificationName,
 670:     );
 671: 
 672:     const snapshotIssuer = getText(
 673:       snapshot.issuingOrganizationName ||
 674:         snapshot.issuerName ||
 675:         item.issuingOrganizationName,
 676:     );
 677: 
 678:     const snapshotType = getText(
 679:       snapshot.certificationType || snapshot.type || item.certificationType,
 680:     );
 681: 
 682:     const snapshotState = getText(
 683:       snapshot.credentialState || snapshot.state || item.credentialState,
 684:     );
 685: 
 686:     if (!snapshotName) {
 687:       collector.addError(
 688:         `${snapshotField}.name`,
 689:         "Store the Certification name snapshot with this relationship.",
 690:         "certification_snapshot_required",
 691:       );
 692:     }
 693: 
 694:     validateTextLength(
 695:       collector,
 696:       `${snapshotField}.name`,
 697:       snapshotName,
 698:       CERTIFICATION_SNAPSHOT_LIMITS.name,
 699:       "Certification snapshot name",
 700:     );
 701: 
 702:     validateTextLength(
 703:       collector,
 704:       `${snapshotField}.issuingOrganizationName`,
 705:       snapshotIssuer,
 706:       CERTIFICATION_SNAPSHOT_LIMITS.issuingOrganizationName,
 707:       "Certification issuer snapshot",
 708:     );
 709: 
 710:     validateTextLength(
 711:       collector,
 712:       `${snapshotField}.certificationType`,
 713:       snapshotType,
 714:       CERTIFICATION_SNAPSHOT_LIMITS.certificationType,
 715:       "Certification type snapshot",
 716:     );
 717: 
 718:     validateTextLength(
 719:       collector,
 720:       `${snapshotField}.credentialState`,
 721:       snapshotState,
 722:       CERTIFICATION_SNAPSHOT_LIMITS.credentialState,
 723:       "Certification state snapshot",
 724:     );
 725: 
 726:     if (snapshotState && !VALID_CERTIFICATION_STATES.has(snapshotState)) {
 727:       collector.addError(
 728:         `${snapshotField}.credentialState`,
 729:         "The Certification snapshot must use an active, expired, or planned credential state.",
 730:         "invalid_certification_snapshot_state",
 731:       );
 732:     }
 733: 
 734:     const snapshotIssueDate = getText(snapshot.issueDate);
 735: 
 736:     const snapshotExpirationDate = getText(snapshot.expirationDate);
 737: 
 738:     if (snapshotIssueDate && !isValidDate(snapshotIssueDate)) {
 739:       collector.addError(
 740:         `${snapshotField}.issueDate`,
 741:         "The Certification snapshot has an invalid issue date.",
 742:         "invalid_certification_snapshot_date",
 743:       );
 744:     }
 745: 
 746:     if (snapshotExpirationDate && !isValidDate(snapshotExpirationDate)) {
 747:       collector.addError(
 748:         `${snapshotField}.expirationDate`,
 749:         "The Certification snapshot has an invalid expiration date.",
 750:         "invalid_certification_snapshot_date",
 751:       );
 752:     }
 753: 
 754:     /*
 755:      * Central Certification Library validation only
 756:      * runs when the caller supplies the collection.
 757:      */
 758: 
 759:     if (
 760:       shouldCheckCertifications &&
 761:       !availableCertificationIds.has(certificationId)
 762:     ) {
 763:       collector.addError(
 764:         certificationIdField,
 765:         "This linked Certification no longer exists in the Certification Library.",
 766:         "certification_not_found",
 767:       );
 768:     }
 769: 
 770:     const savedCertification = shouldCheckCertifications
 771:       ? findCertificationById(certifications, certificationId)
 772:       : null;
 773: 
 774:     if (
 775:       savedCertification &&
 776:       snapshotName &&
 777:       normalizeComparisonText(snapshotName) !==
 778:         normalizeComparisonText(savedCertification.name)
 779:     ) {
 780:       collector.addWarning(
 781:         `${snapshotField}.name`,
 782:         `The saved snapshot does not match the current Certification name "${savedCertification.name}". It will be refreshed when the relationship is synchronized.`,
 783:         "stale_certification_snapshot",
 784:       );
 785:     }
 786: 
 787:     if (savedCertification?.status === "archived") {
 788:       collector.addWarning(
 789:         certificationIdField,
 790:         `"${savedCertification.name || snapshotName}" is archived in the Certification Library.`,
 791:         "linked_certification_archived",
 792:       );
 793:     }
 794:   });
 795: 
 796:   validateCertificateEarnedConsistency(
 797:     collector,
 798:     training,
 799:     relationships,
 800:     certifications,
 801:     shouldCheckCertifications,
 802:   );
 803: }
 804: 
 805: /*
 806:  * =========================================
 807:  * Certificate-Earned Consistency
 808:  * =========================================
 809:  */
 810: 
 811: function validateCertificateEarnedConsistency(
 812:   collector,
 813:   training,
 814:   relationships,
 815:   certifications,
 816:   shouldCheckCertifications,
 817: ) {
 818:   const completion = getObject(training.completion);
 819: 
 820:   const certificateEarned = completion.certificateEarned === true;
 821: 
 822:   const credentialId = getText(completion.credentialId);
 823: 
 824:   const credentialUrl = getText(completion.credentialUrl);
 825: 
 826:   const linkedCertifications = relationships
 827:     .map((relationship) => {
 828:       const item = getObject(relationship);
 829: 
 830:       const certificationId = getText(
 831:         item.certificationId || item.certificationRecordId || item.credentialId,
 832:       );
 833: 
 834:       const savedCertification = shouldCheckCertifications
 835:         ? findCertificationById(certifications, certificationId)
 836:         : null;
 837: 
 838:       const snapshot = getObject(item.snapshot);
 839: 
 840:       return {
 841:         certificationId,
 842: 
 843:         certification: savedCertification,
 844: 
 845:         state: getText(
 846:           getCertificationState(savedCertification) ||
 847:             snapshot.credentialState ||
 848:             snapshot.state ||
 849:             item.credentialState,
 850:         ),
 851:       };
 852:     })
 853:     .filter((entry) => entry.certificationId);
 854: 
 855:   const linkedIssuedCertifications = linkedCertifications.filter(
 856:     (entry) => entry.state === "active" || entry.state === "expired",
 857:   );
 858: 
 859:   const linkedPlannedCertifications = linkedCertifications.filter(
 860:     (entry) => entry.state === "planned",
 861:   );
 862: 
 863:   /*
 864:    * An active or expired Certification represents
 865:    * an issued credential, so Training should indicate
 866:    * that a certificate was earned.
 867:    */
 868: 
 869:   if (linkedIssuedCertifications.length > 0 && !certificateEarned) {
 870:     collector.addError(
 871:       "completion.certificateEarned",
 872:       "Mark the certificate as earned because this Training is linked to an active or expired Certification.",
 873:       "linked_certification_not_marked_earned",
 874:     );
 875:   }
 876: 
 877:   /*
 878:    * A Training linked only to planned credentials
 879:    * should not claim the certificate was earned.
 880:    */
 881: 
 882:   if (
 883:     certificateEarned &&
 884:     linkedCertifications.length > 0 &&
 885:     linkedIssuedCertifications.length === 0 &&
 886:     linkedPlannedCertifications.length > 0
 887:   ) {
 888:     collector.addError(
 889:       "completion.certificateEarned",
 890:       "A planned Certification has not been earned yet.",
 891:       "planned_certification_marked_earned",
 892:     );
 893:   }
 894: 
 895:   /*
 896:    * Existing Training records may already contain
 897:    * credential details without a central relationship.
 898:    * Keep this as a warning so those records can still
 899:    * be opened and migrated.
 900:    */
 901: 
 902:   if (certificateEarned && linkedCertifications.length === 0) {
 903:     collector.addWarning(
 904:       "certificationRelationships",
 905:       "This Training indicates that a certificate was earned, but it is not linked to the Certification Library.",
 906:       "earned_certificate_not_linked",
 907:     );
 908:   }
 909: 
 910:   /*
 911:    * Credential information should not be entered when
 912:    * Training explicitly says that no certificate was
 913:    * earned, unless the linked credential is planned.
 914:    */
 915: 
 916:   if (
 917:     !certificateEarned &&
 918:     (credentialId || credentialUrl) &&
 919:     linkedPlannedCertifications.length === 0
 920:   ) {
 921:     collector.addError(
 922:       "completion.certificateEarned",
 923:       "Mark the certificate as earned or remove the credential information.",
 924:       "credential_without_earned_certificate",
 925:     );
 926:   }
 927: 
 928:   /*
 929:    * When linked Certification records are available,
 930:    * compare legacy Training credential fields with the
 931:    * central Certification Library values.
 932:    */
 933: 
 934:   if (shouldCheckCertifications && linkedCertifications.length > 0) {
 935:     const savedCredentialIds = linkedCertifications
 936:       .map((entry) => getText(entry.certification?.credential?.credentialId))
 937:       .filter(Boolean);
 938: 
 939:     const savedVerificationUrls = linkedCertifications
 940:       .map((entry) => getText(entry.certification?.credential?.verificationUrl))
 941:       .filter(Boolean);
 942: 
 943:     if (
 944:       credentialId &&
 945:       savedCredentialIds.length > 0 &&
 946:       !savedCredentialIds.some(
 947:         (savedCredentialId) =>
 948:           normalizeComparisonText(savedCredentialId) ===
 949:           normalizeComparisonText(credentialId),
 950:       )
 951:     ) {
 952:       collector.addWarning(
 953:         "completion.credentialId",
 954:         "The Training credential ID does not match any linked Certification record.",
 955:         "credential_id_relationship_mismatch",
 956:       );
 957:     }
 958: 
 959:     if (
 960:       credentialUrl &&
 961:       savedVerificationUrls.length > 0 &&
 962:       !savedVerificationUrls.some(
 963:         (savedUrl) =>
 964:           normalizeComparisonText(savedUrl) ===
 965:           normalizeComparisonText(credentialUrl),
 966:       )
 967:     ) {
 968:       collector.addWarning(
 969:         "completion.credentialUrl",
 970:         "The Training credential URL does not match any linked Certification record.",
 971:         "credential_url_relationship_mismatch",
 972:       );
 973:     }
 974: 
 975:     if (
 976:       certificateEarned &&
 977:       !credentialId &&
 978:       !credentialUrl &&
 979:       savedCredentialIds.length === 0 &&
 980:       savedVerificationUrls.length === 0 &&
 981:       getArray(training.supportingDocuments).length === 0
 982:     ) {
 983:       collector.addWarning(
 984:         "completion.certificateEarned",
 985:         "Consider adding a credential ID, verification URL, or certificate document to the linked Certification.",
 986:         "missing_linked_credential_evidence",
 987:       );
 988:     }
 989:   }
 990: }
 991: 
 992: function validateDocuments(collector, training) {
 993:   const documents = getArray(training.supportingDocuments);
 994:   const identities = new Set();
 995: 
 996:   if (documents.length > TRAINING_FIELD_LIMITS.maximumDocuments) {
 997:     collector.addError(
 998:       "supportingDocuments",
 999:       `Add no more than ${TRAINING_FIELD_LIMITS.maximumDocuments} documents.`,
1000:       "too_many_items",
1001:     );
1002:   }
1003: 
1004:   documents.forEach((document, index) => {
1005:     const item = getObject(document);
1006: 
1007:     if (!getText(item.name)) {
1008:       collector.addError(
1009:         `supportingDocuments.${index}.name`,
1010:         "Enter a document name.",
1011:         "required",
1012:       );
1013:     }
1014: 
1015:     if (!getText(item.storageKey) && !getText(item.fileUrl)) {
1016:       collector.addError(
1017:         `supportingDocuments.${index}.storageKey`,
1018:         "The document does not have a storage location.",
1019:         "required",
1020:       );
1021:     }
1022: 
1023:     if (!isValidTrainingDocumentType(item.documentType)) {
1024:       collector.addError(
1025:         `supportingDocuments.${index}.documentType`,
1026:         "Select a valid training document type.",
1027:         "invalid_option",
1028:       );
1029:     }
1030: 
1031:     if (!isValidDocumentVisibility(item.visibility)) {
1032:       collector.addError(
1033:         `supportingDocuments.${index}.visibility`,
1034:         "Select a valid document visibility.",
1035:         "invalid_option",
1036:       );
1037:     }
1038: 
1039:     if (Number(item.fileSize) > DOCUMENT_FILE_LIMITS.maximumFileSizeBytes) {
1040:       collector.addError(
1041:         `supportingDocuments.${index}.fileSize`,
1042:         "The document exceeds the maximum file size.",
1043:         "file_too_large",
1044:       );
1045:     }
1046: 
1047:     const identity =
1048:       getText(item.storageKey) ||
1049:       getText(item.fileUrl) ||
1050:       getText(item.fileName);
1051: 
1052:     if (identity && identities.has(identity)) {
1053:       collector.addError(
1054:         `supportingDocuments.${index}.fileName`,
1055:         "This document has already been added.",
1056:         "duplicate",
1057:       );
1058:     }
1059: 
1060:     identities.add(identity);
1061: 
1062:     validateOrder(collector, `supportingDocuments.${index}.order`, item.order);
1063:   });
1064: }
1065: 
1066: function validateSupportingInformation(collector, training) {
1067:   validateTextLength(
1068:     collector,
1069:     "privateInformation.notes",
1070:     training.privateInformation?.notes,
1071:     TRAINING_FIELD_LIMITS.privateNotes,
1072:     "Private notes",
1073:   );
1074: 
1075:   validateTextLength(
1076:     collector,
1077:     "sourceContext",
1078:     training.sourceContext,
1079:     TRAINING_FIELD_LIMITS.sourceContext,
1080:     "Source context",
1081:   );
1082: 
1083:   if (
1084:     !getText(training.description) &&
1085:     getArray(training.topics).length === 0 &&
1086:     getArray(training.learningOutcomes).length === 0
1087:   ) {
1088:     collector.addWarning(
1089:       "description",
1090:       "Consider adding a description, topic, or learning outcome.",
1091:       "limited_supporting_information",
1092:     );
1093:   }
1094: }
1095: 
1096: export function createTrainingFieldErrorMap(errors = []) {
1097:   return errors.reduce((fieldErrors, error) => {
1098:     if (!fieldErrors[error.field]) {
1099:       fieldErrors[error.field] = error.message;
1100:     }
1101: 
1102:     return fieldErrors;
1103:   }, {});
1104: }
1105: 
1106: export function validateTraining(
1107:   trainingValue,
1108:   { certifications = null } = {},
1109: ) {
1110:   const training = getObject(trainingValue);
1111: 
1112:   const collector = createCollector();
1113: 
1114:   validateRequiredInformation(collector, training);
1115:   validateOptions(collector, training);
1116:   validatePrimaryFields(collector, training);
1117:   validateDates(collector, training);
1118:   validateCompletion(collector, training);
1119:   validateLocation(collector, training);
1120:   validateTrainingDetails(collector, training);
1121:   validateSkillRelationships(collector, training);
1122: 
1123:   validateCertificationRelationships(
1124:     collector,
1125:     training,
1126:     getArray(certifications),
1127:     Array.isArray(certifications),
1128:   );
1129: 
1130:   validateDocuments(collector, training);
1131:   validateSupportingInformation(collector, training);
1132: 
1133:   return {
1134:     isValid: collector.errors.length === 0,
1135:     errors: collector.errors,
1136:     warnings: collector.warnings,
1137:     fieldErrors: createTrainingFieldErrorMap(collector.errors),
1138:   };
1139: }
1140: 
1141: export function assertValidTraining(training, options = {}) {
1142:   const validation = validateTraining(training, options);
1143: 
1144:   if (validation.isValid) {
1145:     return validation;
1146:   }
1147: 
1148:   const error = new Error(
1149:     "The training record contains invalid or incomplete information.",
1150:   );
1151: 
1152:   error.name = "TrainingValidationError";
1153:   error.code = "TRAINING_VALIDATION_FAILED";
1154:   error.status = 400;
1155:   error.publicMessage =
1156:     "Correct the highlighted training information before saving.";
1157:   error.validation = validation;
1158:   error.errors = validation.errors;
1159:   error.warnings = validation.warnings;
1160:   error.fieldErrors = validation.fieldErrors;
1161: 
1162:   throw error;
1163: }
1164: 
1165: export function getTrainingValidationSummary(training, options = {}) {
1166:   const validation = validateTraining(training, options);
1167: 
1168:   if (!validation.isValid) {
1169:     return `${validation.errors.length} ${
1170:       validation.errors.length === 1 ? "problem must" : "problems must"
1171:     } be corrected before saving.`;
1172:   }
1173: 
1174:   if (validation.warnings.length > 0) {
1175:     return `Ready to save with ${validation.warnings.length} ${
1176:       validation.warnings.length === 1 ? "suggestion" : "suggestions"
1177:     } for improvement.`;
1178:   }
1179: 
1180:   return "This training record is complete and ready to save.";
1181: }
